## 2570002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うおっ！<end>"
EN: "Whoa!<end>"
KO: "우왓!<end>"

## 2570003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>大丈夫だ。案ずることはない。<line-break>軽い…発作のようなものだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...? I'm all right. There's no cause for concern. It's just... a minor attack of some kind.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 괜찮다. 걱정할 것 없어. 가벼운… 발작 같은 거다.<end>"

## 2570004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…しばらく。<line-break>ひとりに…しておいてくれ。<line-break>…じきに治まる。<end>"
EN: "Leave me... alone for a while. It will pass soon.<end>"
KO: "…잠시만. 혼자… 있게 해 줘. …곧 가라앉을 거야.<end>"

## 2570005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むっ、おおっ、くうっ！<end>"
EN: "Ngh, aaagh, grrrgh!<end>"
KO: "으윽, 오오, 크윽!<end>"

## 2570006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はあっ、はあっ、はあっ…。<line-break>…………。<line-break>　<line-break>…聞こえなかったのか！？<line-break>ひとりにしろと言ったはずだぞ！<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... hah... Didn't you hear me!? I told you to leave me alone!<end>"
KO: "하아, 하아, 하아…. ………… 못 들었나!? 혼자 있게 하라고 했을 텐데!<end>"

## 2570007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ、バレてたか。<end>"
EN: "Damn, you knew I was here.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 들켰나.<end>"

## 2570008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何の…用だ！？<line-break>見ての通り…取り込み中だ…！<end>"
EN: "What do you... want!? As you can see... I'm rather occupied...!<end>"
KO: "무슨… 용건이지!? 보다시피… 지금 바쁘다…!<end>"

## 2570010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理することはない。<line-break>限界が来たら俺を襲いな。<line-break>貸しにしといてやる。<end>"
EN: "No need to push yourself. If you reach your limit, attack me. You can owe me one.<end>"
KO: "무리할 것 없어. 한계가 오면 나를 덮쳐. 빚으로 남겨 두지.<end>"

## 2570011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…去れ。手助けなど無用…！<line-break>要らぬ世話だ…！<end>"
EN: "Go. I need no help...! Spare me your meddling...!<end>"
KO: "…가라. 도움 따윈 필요 없다…! 쓸데없는 참견이다…!<end>"

## 2570012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だとさ。<line-break>どうするよ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Well, that's the answer. What will you do, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…그렇다는데. 어떻게 할래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2570013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…！ずっとそこで…見ていたのか！？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...! Have you been watching from there... all this time!?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…! 계속 거기서… 보고 있었나!?<end>"

## 2570014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…。要らぬ世話焼きが<line-break>２人もいるとなると…さすがに<line-break>不様な姿は…見せられぬか…。<line-break>ぐあっ！<end>"
EN: "Heh... With two meddlesome busybodies here, I can hardly let myself be seen in such a sorry state... Gah!<end>"
KO: "후…. 쓸데없이 참견하는 녀석이 둘이나 있으니… 아무래도 추한 모습은… 보일 수 없겠군…. 크윽!<end>"

## 2570015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じきに夜が明ける。<line-break>もう少しの辛抱だぜ。<end>"
EN: "Dawn will be here soon. Just hold on a little longer.<end>"
KO: "곧 날이 밝는다. 조금만 더 버텨.<end>"

## 2570016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たいした根性だ。<line-break>本能の衝動を、理性と意志の力で<line-break>抑えこみやがった。<end>"
EN: "Now that's grit: beating back an instinctive urge with reason and sheer force of will.<end>"
KO: "대단한 근성이군. 본능의 충동을 이성과 의지의 힘으로억눌러 버렸어.<end>"

## 2570017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次もこういくとは限らん。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>おかしな奴らだ。<line-break>なぜ、こんな危険な状態の<line-break>ダルケニスを見守る？<end>"
EN: "It may not go so well next time. You are strange. Why would you stay and watch over a Darkenith in such a dangerous state?<end>"
KO: "다음에도 이렇게 될 거란 보장은 없다. 이상한 녀석들이군. 왜 이렇게 위험한 상태의 다르케니스를 지켜보는 거지?<end>"

## 2570018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間を見守るのに<line-break>理由なんて要るのかい？<line-break>おかしいのはお前さんのほうだ。<end>"
EN: "Do we need a reason to watch over a companion? You're the strange one here.<end>"
KO: "동료를 지켜보는 데 이유가 필요해? 이상한 건 네 쪽이야.<end>"

## 2570019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのか？　ダルケニスの俺を<line-break>仲間と呼んで？<line-break>　<line-break>いつか後悔することになるかも<line-break>しれんぞ？<end>"
EN: "Are you sure you want to call me, a Darkenith, your companion? You may come to regret it someday.<end>"
KO: "괜찮겠나? 다르케니스인 나를 동료라고 불러도? 언젠가 후회하게 될지도 모른다.<end>"

## 2570020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念だったな。<line-break>反省はするが後悔はしない主義でね。<line-break>たぶん<value:$28>も同じだぜ。<line-break>なあ？<end>"
EN: "Too bad. I believe in learning from my mistakes, not regretting them. I bet <value:$28> feels the same. Right?<end>"
KO: "아쉽게 됐군. 반성은 해도 후회는 안 하는 주의라서 말이야. 아마 <value:$28>도 같겠지. 그렇지?<end>"

## 2570021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec257-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新月のレムオン用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "신월의 레무온용 종료<end>"

## 2580000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用誕生日イベント用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 생일 이벤트용<end>"

## 2580001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう、<value:$28>。<line-break><line-break><line-break>誕生日のお祝いすると、<line-break>ロストールにいたころを思い出すわ。<line-break><line-break>みんな元気でやってるかしら？<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>. Celebrating your birthday reminds me of when I was in Rostorl. I wonder how everyone is doing.<end>"
KO: "축하해, <value:$28>. 생일을 축하하니 로스톨에 있던 때가 생각나네. 다들 잘 지내고 있으려나?<end>"

## 2580002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう、<value:$28>。<line-break><line-break><line-break>この１年…いろいろあったけど<line-break>あなたがいてくれたおかげで<line-break>乗り切れたんだと思う…。<line-break>これからの１年もよろしくね？<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>. So much has happened this past year, but I think I made it through because you were there. I hope you'll stay with me through the year ahead too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "축하해, <value:$28>. 지난 1년… 여러 일이 있었지만 네가 있어 줬기에 헤쳐 나올 수 있었던 것 같아…. 앞으로 1년도 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2580003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、すべてがめまぐるしくて<line-break>自分の誕生日も<line-break>忘れておったのではないか？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Everything has been such a whirlwind, I suspect you forgot your own birthday.<end>"
KO: "후후, 모든 일이 숨 가쁘게 돌아가서 자기 생일도 잊고 있었던 것 아니냐?<end>"

## 2580004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまで<line-break>冒険と戦争に明け暮れた人生で<line-break>我が子の誕生日も祝ってやれなんだ。<line-break>今日、お主の生まれた日を<line-break>祝ってやれることが…なんというか<line-break>わしには、この上なく、うれしい。<end>"
EN: "I have spent my life embroiled in adventures and wars, and could not even celebrate my own child's birthday. To be able to celebrate the day you were born... Well, I cannot quite put it into words. It brings me greater joy than anything.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 모험과 전쟁에만 매달린 인생이라 내 아이 생일조차 축하해 주지 못했다. 오늘 네가 태어난 날을 축하해 줄 수 있다는 게… 뭐랄까, 나한테는 더없이 기쁘구나.<end>"

## 2580005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう！<line-break>さ、今日は、<value:$28>の<line-break>おごりでご馳走かも！<line-break>生まれてきてくれて<line-break>ありがとね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday! Now then, perhaps we'll have a feast today, courtesy of <value:$28>! Thank you for being born, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "축하해! 자, 오늘은 <value:$28>이 한턱내는 걸로 잔치일지도! 태어나 줘서 고마워, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2580006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>誕生日おめでとう。<line-break>　<line-break>お祝いに、<line-break>今研究中の魔法を見せちゃおうかな？<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>. Maybe I'll celebrate by showing you the spell I'm working on.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 생일 축하해. 선물로 지금 연구 중인 마법을 보여 줄까?<end>"

## 2580007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くーっ！　誕生日がめでたいぜ！<line-break>これからもよろしくな！<end>"
EN: "Ahh! A birthday is cause for celebration! Let's keep at it together!<end>"
KO: "크으! 생일이라니 경사로군! 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다!<end>"

## 2580008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、おめでとう！<line-break>こういうのって、<line-break>やっぱ友情を感じるよな。<line-break>俺の熱い魂が騒ぐぜ！<end>"
EN: "Congratulations, <value:$28>! Times like this really make you feel the bonds of friendship. My fiery soul is stirring!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 축하해! 이런 건 역시 우정이 느껴지지. 내 뜨거운 혼이 들끓는군!<end>"

## 2580009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>誕生日おめでと！<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 생일 축하해!<end>"

## 2580010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでと！<line-break><value:$28>と旅ができて、<line-break>ボク…ほんと、うれしいよ！<line-break>これからもずっと<line-break>一緒にいられたら、いいのにな…。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday! I'm so glad I get to travel with you, <value:$28>. I only wish we could stay together forever.<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해! <value:$28>과 여행할 수 있어서 나… 정말 기뻐! 앞으로도 계속 함께 있을 수 있으면 좋겠다….<end>"

## 2580011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>誕生日だからといって、<line-break>浮かれないで。<line-break>まだまだ、やるべきことがあるわ。<line-break>本当のお祝いができるのは、<line-break>それが済んだ後よ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, don't get carried away just because it's your birthday. We still have a great deal to do. We can celebrate properly once it's all over.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 생일이라고 들뜨지 마. 아직 해야 할 일이 많이 남았어. 진짜 축하는 그게 끝난 뒤에야 할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2580012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、誕生日なのね。<line-break>今日は、ゆっくり休むといいわ。<line-break>　<line-break>明日からは、また、<line-break>戦いの日々が待っているから…<end>"
EN: "So it's your birthday. You should rest while you can today. Tomorrow, the fighting begins again...<end>"
KO: "그래, 생일이구나. 오늘은 푹 쉬도록 해. 내일부터는 또 싸움의 나날이 기다리고 있으니까….<end>"

## 2580013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでと、<value:$28>。<line-break>来年もお互い生きてるといいやね。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>. Let's hope we're both still alive next year.<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해, <value:$28>. 내년에도 서로 살아 있으면 좋겠네.<end>"

## 2580014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はさ、こんなご時世だから、<line-break>みんな集まってお祝いって<line-break>わけにはいかないじゃん？<line-break>だから今日はパーッとやってあげるよ。<line-break>パーッとね！<end>"
EN: "With the way things are now, we can't exactly get everyone together to celebrate, can we? So today, I'll give you a proper blowout. A real blowout!<end>"
KO: "요즘 같은 세상이라 다 같이 모여 축하할 순 없잖아? 그러니까 오늘은 화끈하게 축하해 줄게. 화끈하게!<end>"

## 2580015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ま、これからもよろしくたのむ。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>. Well, I'm counting on you from here on out.<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해, <value:$28>. 뭐, 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 2580016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の背中をお前さんに預けて<line-break>安心してられるのも<line-break>この日のおかげってわけだ。<line-break>俺はこの日に感謝してるぜ。<line-break>もちろん、お前さんにもな。<line-break>…ありがとよ。<end>"
EN: "It's thanks to this day that I can trust you to watch my back. I'm grateful for it―and for you, of course. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "내 등을 네게 맡기고 안심할 수 있는 것도 이날 덕분이라는 거지. 난 이날에 감사하고 있어. 물론 너에게도 말이야. …고맙다.<end>"

## 2580017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでとう。<line-break>今日だけは、ゆっくり休むといいわ。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday. Just for today, take it easy and rest.<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해. 오늘만큼은 푹 쉬도록 해.<end>"

## 2580018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、こうして誕生日を祝うなんて<line-break>何年振りか…<line-break><line-break>誕生日を祝える相手など、<line-break>私にはいなかったから…<line-break><line-break><value:$28>、おめでとう。<end>"
EN: "Heh. How many years has it been since I last celebrated a birthday like this? There was no one whose birthday I could celebrate. Congratulations, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후, 이렇게 생일을 축하하는 게 몇 년 만인지… 생일을 축하해 줄 상대 같은 건 내게 없었으니까… <value:$28>, 축하해.<end>"

## 2580019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>フッ、おめでたいな。<line-break>年だけではなく、<line-break>実力もちゃんと上がっているのか？<end>"
EN: "A birthday? Hmph. How delightful. Have your skills grown along with your age?<end>"
KO: "생일? 흥, 축하할 일이군. 나이만이 아니라 실력도 제대로 늘고 있는 건가?<end>"

## 2580020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日か…<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>今日ぐらいはゆっくりするといい。<line-break>明日からはまた戦いになる。<line-break>　<line-break>それと…<line-break>おめでとう<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Your birthday... Take it easy, at least for today. Tomorrow, the fighting begins again. And... happy birthday, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "생일인가… 오늘 정도는 푹 쉬어. 내일부터는 또 싸움이다. 그리고… 축하한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2580021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでとう！<line-break>俺、姉ちゃんの歌を作ったんだ。<line-break>お祝いに歌うよ！<line-break>あ…ごめ…殴らないで！<line-break>う、歌なんて歌いません！<line-break>ち、調子乗ってごめんなさい…。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday! I wrote a song about you, Sis. I'll sing it to celebrate! Ah... sorry... Don't hit me! I-I won't sing anything! Sorry I got carried away...<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해! 나 누나 노래를 만들었어. 축하로 불러 줄게! 아… 미안… 때리지 마! 노, 노래 같은 건 안 부를게! 나, 까불어서 미안해….<end>"

## 2580022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そっか。<line-break>俺たちがノーブルを離れて、<line-break>もうずいぶん経ったんだな。<line-break>…いつか戻ろうな。<line-break>父さんの畑に。<end>"
EN: "Right... It really has been a long time since we left Noble. Let's go back someday. Back to Dad's field.<end>"
KO: "…그렇구나. 우리가 노블을 떠난 지도 벌써 꽤 됐네. …언젠가 돌아가자. 아버지의 밭으로.<end>"

## 2580023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。<line-break>今日は祝宴じゃ！<line-break>めいっぱい飲もうかの。<end>"
EN: "Good! Today, we feast! Let us drink our fill.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 오늘은 잔치다! 실컷 마셔 보자꾸나.<end>"

## 2580024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、誕生日か…。<line-break>お主の成長がうれしくもあるいっぽう<line-break>人の子は寿命が短いから寂しくもある。<line-break>まあよい！<line-break>今日は思い切り飲んで、<line-break>お主の成長を喜ぶことに専念しようぞ！<end>"
EN: "So it is your birthday. I rejoice to see how you have grown, yet it saddens me that human lives are so short. But no matter! Today we shall drink our fill and devote ourselves to celebrating your growth!<end>"
KO: "그래, 생일인가…. 네 성장이 기쁘면서도 인간의 아이는 수명이 짧아 서글프기도 하구나. 뭐 됐다! 오늘은 마음껏 마시며 네 성장을 기뻐하는 데 전념하자꾸나!<end>"

## 2580025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>誕生日おめでとう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Happy birthday.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 생일 축하해.<end>"

## 2580026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだまだ戦いは続くけど、<line-break>これからもがんばっていこう。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>の誕生日を<line-break>来年もお祝いできるようにね。<end>"
EN: "The fighting isn't over yet, but let's keep doing our best. That way, we can celebrate your birthday again next year, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아직 싸움은 계속되지만 앞으로도 힘내자. 내년에도 <value:$28>의 생일을 축하할 수 있도록 말이야.<end>"

## 2580027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ま、ド下等生物の誕生日なんて<line-break>高貴なエルフの誕生日じゃない日より<line-break>ハッキリ言って下よ！<line-break>だから、高貴で優雅なエルフである<line-break>アタクシの誕生日でない日を<line-break>祝うのよ、<value:$28>～！<end>"
EN: "Well, to be perfectly blunt, the birthday of some utterly inferior creature ranks below any day that isn't the birthday of a noble Elf! So what we're celebrating, <value:$28>, is a day that is not the birthday of the noble and elegant me!<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 최하등생물의 생일 따위는 고귀한 엘프의 생일이 아닌 날보다도 솔직히 아래야! 그러니까 고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 이 몸의 생일이 아닌 날을 축하하는 거야, <value:$28>~!<end>"

## 2580028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつ年取ったくらいでいばらないで！<line-break>高貴なエルフであるアタクシなんて<line-break>もう１６００歳を超えてるのよ～！<line-break>もっとたくさん誕生日を迎えて<line-break>早く高貴なアタクシに近づくのよ！<line-break>いいこと、<value:$28>～！<end>"
EN: "Don't get conceited just because you're one year older! I, a noble Elf, am already over sixteen hundred years old! Have many more birthdays and hurry up and become more like my noble self! Is that clear, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "겨우 한 살 더 먹었다고 으스대지 마! 고귀한 엘프인 아타쿠시는 벌써 1600살도 넘었거든~! 생일을 더 많이 맞아서 얼른 고귀한 아타쿠시에게 가까워지도록 해! 알겠지, <value:$28>~!<end>"

## 2580029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、おめでとう。<line-break>ルルアンタも、とってもうれしいよ。<line-break>これからもよろしくね。<end>"
EN: "Congratulations, <value:$28>. Lulluantha is very happy too. I hope we'll keep getting along.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 축하해. 루루안타도 정말 기뻐. 앞으로도 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 2580030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでとう。<line-break><value:$28>と旅ができて<line-break>ほんと、うれしいな！<line-break>明日からまた元気よく<line-break>旅を続けていこうね。<line-break>ルルアンタはいつも一緒だよ！<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday. I'm so glad I get to travel with you, <value:$28>! Tomorrow, let's set out again with plenty of energy. Lulluantha will always be with you!<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해. <value:$28>과 여행할 수 있어서 정말 기뻐! 내일부터도 힘차게 여행을 계속하자. 루루안타는 언제나 함께야!<end>"

## 2580031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…めでたい。<end>"
EN: "A joyous occasion.<end>"
KO: "…경사로군.<end>"

## 2580032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…年だけではない。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>うぬは、我と勝負したときから<line-break>また一段と成長した。<line-break>　<line-break>再び対する時のため、<line-break>我も…<end>"
EN: "...It is not only your age. Since our contest, you have grown stronger still. To prepare for the day we face each other again, I too...<end>"
KO: "………………. …나이뿐만이 아니다. 그대는 나와 승부했을 때부터 또 한 단계 성장했다. 다시 맞설 때를 위해 나도….<end>"

## 2580033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "特別なことはしてやれないが、<line-break>おめでとうぐらいは言わせてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I can't do anything special for you, but at least let me offer my congratulations.<end>"
KO: "특별한 건 해줄 수 없지만 축하한다는 말 정도는 하게 해줘.<end>"

## 2580034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日ぐらいはゆっくりするといい。<line-break>　<line-break>最近がんばりすぎているようだが、<line-break>あまり無茶をするな。<line-break>　<line-break>来年も、お前の誕生日を祝いたいからな。<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday, <value:$28>. Take it easy, at least for today. You seem to have been pushing yourself too hard lately, so don't do anything reckless. I want to celebrate your birthday again next year.<end>"
KO: "축하한다, <value:$28>. 오늘 정도는 느긋하게 쉬어. 요즘 너무 무리하는 것 같으니 너무 지나치진 마라. 내년에도 네 생일을 축하하고 싶으니까.<end>"

## 2580035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私からも祝わせてもらうぞ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to offer my congratulations as well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나도 축하해 주지. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2580036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は自分の誕生日を<line-break>幾度となく呪ったが<line-break>君のは祝える…心からな。<line-break>それは、たぶん<line-break>私にとっても幸せなことだ。<line-break>君と君の生まれた日に、感謝する。<end>"
EN: "I have cursed my own birthday more times than I can count, but yours I can celebrate, and with all my heart. Perhaps that brings me happiness too. I am grateful for you, and for the day you were born.<end>"
KO: "나는 내 생일을 수없이 저주했지만 네 생일은 축하할 수 있어… 진심으로. 그건 아마 나에게도 행복한 일이겠지. 너와 네가 태어난 날에 감사한다.<end>"

## 2580037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かの誕生日を祝うなんて、<line-break>しばらく無かったな。<line-break>おめでとう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It's been a while since I celebrated anyone's birthday. Congratulations, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "누군가의 생일을 축하하는 건 오랜만이군. 축하해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2580038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>という生命が生まれ<line-break>今日、ここまで成長したのだな。<line-break>なんだか、感慨深いよ。<line-break>これからも<line-break>お前の成長を見守ることが<line-break>できたらいいと思っている。<end>"
EN: "The life called <value:$28> came into this world, and now you've grown this far. It gives me a strange sense of wonder. I hope I can continue to watch over your growth.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>라는 생명이 태어나 오늘 여기까지 성장한 거구나. 왠지 감회가 깊어. 앞으로도 네 성장을 지켜볼 수 있으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 2580039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天が巡り、星座がまた<line-break>汝が生まれた時と同じ配置となった。<line-break>汝に天空神の加護があらんことを。<line-break>天空神ノトゥーンの誕生日の経文よ。<line-break>わしからじゃありがたみは薄いだろうが<line-break>一応、利益はあるぞ。<end>"
EN: "The heavens have turned, and the constellations once more stand as they did at your birth. May the blessing of the Sky God be upon you. That was the Sky God Notun's birthday scripture. It may not carry much weight coming from me, but it still has some efficacy.<end>"
KO: "하늘이 순환하여 별자리가 다시 네가 태어났을 때와 같은 배치가 되었다. 그대에게 천공신의 가호가 있기를. 천공신 노툰의 생일 경문이다. 내가 해주면 감흥은 덜하겠지만 그래도 효험은 있을 게다.<end>"

## 2580040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天が巡り、星座がまた<line-break>汝が生まれた時と同じ配置となった。<line-break>汝に天空神の加護があらんことを。<line-break>ま、誕生日じゃなくとも、<line-break>天も星々も、お主が生まれたことを<line-break>いつも祝福しとるんじゃがな。<end>"
EN: "The heavens have turned, and the constellations once more stand as they did at your birth. May the blessing of the Sky God be upon you. Of course, birthday or not, the heavens and stars are always celebrating your birth.<end>"
KO: "하늘이 돌고 별자리가 다시 네가 태어났을 때와 같은 배치가 되었구나. 하늘신의 가호가 네게 있기를. 뭐, 생일이 아니어도 하늘과 별들은 네가 태어난 걸 늘 축복하고 있지만 말이지.<end>"

## 2580041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでとうございます。<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "A very happy birthday to you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "생일 축하드립니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 2580042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>おめでとうございます。<line-break><line-break>ふふっ、感情を抑える必要もないのに、<line-break>どうしても<line-break>いつものようになってしまう…。<line-break>私の素直なお祝いの気持ち…<line-break>それが伝わっていればいいのですが。<end>"
EN: "My warmest congratulations, <value:$28>. Hehe. There is no need to restrain my feelings, yet somehow I still fall into my usual manner. I only hope my sincere wish to celebrate you has come through.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 축하드립니다. 후후, 감정을 억누를 필요도 없는데 아무래도 평소처럼 되어 버리네요…. 제 솔직한 축하의 마음… 그게 전해졌으면 좋겠습니다.<end>"

## 2580043

KO: "<value:$28>. 생일 축하해.<end>"

## 2580044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日おめでとう。<line-break>今まで無事にやってこられたのも、<line-break><value:$28>のおかげかな。<line-break>これからの戦いは大変だけど、<line-break><value:$28>や仲間と一緒なら、<line-break>きっと大丈夫だよね！<end>"
EN: "Happy birthday. I suppose it's thanks to you that we've made it this far safely, <value:$28>. The battles ahead will be hard, but as long as <value:$28> and our companions are together, I'm sure everything will be all right!<end>"
KO: "생일 축하해. 지금까지 무사히 지낼 수 있었던 것도 <value:$28> 덕분이려나. 앞으로 싸움은 힘들겠지만 <value:$28>와 동료들이 함께라면 분명 괜찮을 거야!<end>"

## 2580045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、記念に、<line-break><value:$28>の歌を作るよ。<end>"
EN: "All right! To mark the occasion, I'll write a song about <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 기념으로 <value:$28>의 노래를 만들게.<end>"

## 2580046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日はいいね。<line-break>自分のでも、仲間のでもうれしいよ。<line-break><line-break>毎日が誰かの誕生日になるくらいに、<line-break>仲間がいっぱい増えるといいな。<line-break>ぼく、そう思うよ。<line-break>そしたら、ぼく、<line-break>毎日、お祝いの歌を歌っちゃうのに。<end>"
EN: "Birthdays are wonderful. Mine or a companion's, they always make me happy. I hope we gain so many companions that every day is someone's birthday. I really do. Then I could sing a birthday song every single day.<end>"
KO: "생일은 좋아. 내 생일이든 동료 생일이든 기쁘잖아. 매일 누군가의 생일이 될 만큼 동료가 많이 늘면 좋겠어. 난 그렇게 생각해. 그러면 매일 축하 노래를 부를 텐데.<end>"

## 2580047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生した日付とは<line-break>祝うべきもの…なのですか？<end>"
EN: "Is the date of one's birth... something to celebrate?<end>"
KO: "태어난 날짜라는 건 축하해야 하는 것…입니까?<end>"

## 2580048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日…はよくわかりませんが<line-break><value:$28>様が生まれた事実は<line-break>私の心を暖かくします。<line-break>これが、誕生日を祝うことなのですね。<line-break>本物のフレアも誰かの誕生日を<line-break>祝ったことがあったのでしょうか？<line-break>その時は、きっと今の私のような<line-break>気持ちになれたのでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "I do not entirely understand birthdays, but the fact that you were born warms my heart, <value:$28>. So this is what it means to celebrate a birthday. I wonder if the real Flare ever celebrated someone's birthday. If so, perhaps she felt as I do now.<end>"
KO: "생일…은 잘 모르겠습니다만 <value:$28>님이 태어났다는 사실은 제 마음을 따뜻하게 합니다. 이것이 생일을 축하한다는 것이군요. 진짜 플레어도 누군가의 생일을 축하한 적이 있었을까요? 그때는 분명 지금의 저와 같은 기분이었겠지요.<end>"

## 2580049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、誕生日おめでとうございます。<line-break><value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "H-happy birthday, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "새, 생일 축하합니다. <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 2580050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、私…<line-break>道具があればケーキ焼いたんですけど…<line-break>　<line-break>でも、ケーキが無くても<line-break>気持ちさえあれば、<line-break>ちゃんと誕生日お祝いできますよね！？<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>おめでとうございます。<end>"
EN: "I-I would have baked a cake if I'd had the tools... But even without a cake, as long as the feeling is there, we can still celebrate a birthday properly, can't we?! Happy birthday, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저, 저… 도구만 있었으면 케이크를 구웠을 텐데…. 그래도 케이크가 없어도 마음만 있으면 제대로 생일을 축하할 수 있겠죠!? <value:$28> 씨, 축하드려요.<end>"

## 2580051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>誕生日おめでとう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Happy birthday.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 생일 축하해.<end>"

## 2580052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕も昔、姉さんに<line-break>祝ってもらったことがあるよ。<line-break>　<line-break>とてもうれしかったと記憶している。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>今日は僕が、<line-break><value:$28>のお祝いをするよ。<line-break>　<line-break>誕生日、おめでとう。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, my older sister celebrated my birthday for me. I remember how happy it made me. Today, I will celebrate yours, <value:$28>. Happy birthday.<end>"
KO: "나도 옛날에 누나에게 축하받은 적이 있어. 정말 기뻤다고 기억해. 오늘은 내가 <value:$28>를 축하해 줄게. 생일 축하해.<end>"

## 2580053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日なのね。<line-break>おめでとう。<line-break><value:$28>は何歳になったの？<end>"
EN: "So it's your birthday. Congratulations. How old are you now, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "생일이구나. 축하해. <value:$28>는 몇 살이 됐어?<end>"

## 2580054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誕生日なのね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>こんなに素直に誕生日を<line-break>お祝いできるなんて、<line-break>ちょっと前までは考えられなかったわ。<line-break>おめでとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>これからもよろしくね。<end>"
EN: "So it's your birthday. Not long ago, I could never have imagined celebrating one so sincerely. Happy birthday, <value:$28>. I hope we'll continue to get along.<end>"
KO: "생일이구나. 이렇게 솔직하게 생일을 축하할 수 있게 되다니 얼마 전까지만 해도 상상도 못 했어. 축하해, <value:$28>. 앞으로도 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 2580055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "Congratulations, <value:$28>. I'm counting on you from here on out.<end>"
KO: "축하한다, <value:$28>. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 2580056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "普通に誕生日を祝うというのは、<line-break>普通の感覚を失わないでいるためには<line-break>いいことさ。<line-break>「無限のソウル」と呼ばれ続け<line-break>すべての厄介ごとを押しつけられ続け<line-break>いつしか「普通」がわかんなくなる。<line-break>世界はひとりで背負うもんじゃない、<line-break>そんなことも見失うようになる。<line-break>それはもう英雄じゃなくて怪物だよ。<line-break>だから俺は、お前の誕生日を<line-break>普通の言葉で祝ってやりたいんだ。<line-break>おめでとう…って。<end>"
EN: "Celebrating a birthday in the ordinary way helps you hold on to an ordinary person's perspective. When everyone keeps calling you the Infinite Soul and saddling you with every last problem, sooner or later you forget what “ordinary” even means. You even lose sight of the fact that the world isn't something one person should bear alone. At that point, you're no hero. You're a monster. That's why I want to celebrate your birthday in ordinary words. So... happy birthday.<end>"
KO: "평범하게 생일을 축하하는 건 평범한 감각을 잃지 않기 위해 좋은 일이야. '무한의 소울'이라 계속 불리고 온갖 골칫거리를 떠맡다 보면 어느새 '평범함'이 뭔지 모르게 되지. 세상은 혼자 짊어지는 게 아니라는 사실조차 놓치게 돼. 그러면 영웅이 아니라 괴물이야. 그래서 난 네 생일을 평범한 말로 축하해 주고 싶어. 축하해…라고.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2580080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は <value:$28>様が<line-break>この世に生を受けた日と<line-break>同じ日付です。<end>"
EN: "Today's date is the same as the day you came into this world, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 <value:$28>님이 이 세상에 태어난 날과 같은 날짜입니다.<end>"

## 2580081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec258-part99.toml

EN: "If I remember correctly, today is your birthday, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2590000

EN: ""
KO: "통신기 이벤트용 시작<end>"

## 2590001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日は仲間を変更しにきたのですか？<line-break> <line-break>仲間を変更するたびに、<line-break>ここまで来るのは大変でしょうから、<line-break>これを渡しておきましょう。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. Have you come to change your companions today? It must be a nuisance coming all this way whenever you want to change your party, so I'll give you this.<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요, <value:$28>. 오늘은 동료를 바꾸러 오셨나요? 동료를 바꿀 때마다 여기까지 오는 건 힘들 테니 이걸 드릴게요.<end>"

## 2590003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモのおもちゃにするよりは<line-break>あなたに使ってもらうほうが<line-break>有意義でしょう。<end>"
EN: "It will serve a better purpose in your hands than as one of Nemo's toys.<end>"
KO: "네모의 장난감으로 두는 것보단 당신이 쓰는 편이 더 유익하겠죠.<end>"

## 2590004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "使い方は簡単です。<line-break>仲間の入れ替えをしたいときには<line-break>これで私に話しかけてください。<line-break>そうすれば、私は仲間の方を<line-break>そちらに送ったり、あるいは<line-break>別れさせたりできます。<end>"
EN: "It's simple to use. Whenever you want to change your companions, contact me with it. I can then send companions to you or have them leave your party.<end>"
KO: "사용법은 간단합니다. 동료를 바꾸고 싶을 때 이걸로 제게 말을 걸어 주세요. 그러면 제가 동료를 그쪽으로 보내거나 헤어지게 할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 2590005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫です。見た目より頑丈ですから、<line-break>壊れる心配はまずありません。<line-break>　<line-break>他の道具と同じように<line-break>何度でも使えます。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. It's sturdier than it looks, so there's little chance of it breaking. Like your other items, you can use it as many times as you like.<end>"
KO: "괜찮습니다. 보기보다 튼튼해서 망가질 걱정은 거의 없습니다. 다른 도구처럼 몇 번이고 사용할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 2590006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただし、ひとつだけ問題があります。<line-break>この通信機でも、<line-break>つながらない場所がありましてね。<line-break>結界の張られている部屋や、<line-break>特殊な力が作用している場所では<line-break>使えないんですよ。<line-break>まあ、それを差し引いても<line-break>便利な道具ですから<line-break>持っていて損はないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "There is just one problem. Even this communicator cannot reach me from certain places. It won't work in rooms protected by barriers or in places affected by unusual forces. Still, despite that limitation, it's a useful device and well worth keeping.<end>"
KO: "다만 한 가지 문제가 있습니다. 이 통신기로도 연결되지 않는 장소가 있거든요. 결계가 쳐진 방이나 특수한 힘이 작용하는 곳에서는 사용할 수 없습니다. 그래도 그런 점을 감안해도 편리한 도구이니 가지고 있어서 손해 볼 일은 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 2590007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと<value:$28>の<line-break>役に立つと思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sure it will prove useful to you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "분명 <value:$28>에게 도움이 될 거예요.<end>"

## 2590008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec259-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "通信機イベント用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "통신기 이벤트용 끝<end>"

## 2600000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベント01用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질리온&이샤 이벤트01용 시작<end>"

## 2600001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未練かな、やっぱり…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>何度、ここに来ても<line-break>あいつがいるわけないって<line-break>わかってるのに…。<end>"
EN: "I guess I still can't let go... I know full well the one I'm looking for won't be here, no matter how many times I come back...<end>"
KO: "미련인가, 역시…. 몇 번을 여기 와도 그 녀석이 있을 리 없다는 걸 알고 있는데….<end>"

## 2600002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………！<line-break>…この感覚！？<end>"
EN: "...! This feeling?!<end>"
KO: "………! …이 감각은!?<end>"

## 2600003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この感覚…間違いない。<line-break>無限のソウル…。<end>"
EN: "There's no mistaking this feeling. An Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "이 감각… 틀림없어. 무한의 소울….<end>"

## 2600004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。あなたが<line-break>ちょっと知っている人に似てたから…。<line-break>あたしはイーシャ。あなたは？<end>"
EN: "Sorry. You looked a little like someone I know... I'm Iria. And you?<end>"
KO: "미안해. 네가 아는 사람과 조금 닮아서…. 나는 이샤야. 너는?<end>"

## 2600005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。冒険者なのね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... So you're an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 모험자구나.<end>"

## 2600006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうどいいわ。今、私ひとりなの。<line-break>一緒に組まない？<line-break>　<line-break>自分で言うのも何だけど、<line-break>そこいらの冒険者より<line-break>よっぽど役に立つと思うわ。<end>"
EN: "Perfect timing. I'm on my own right now. Want to team up? Not to boast, but I think I'd be far more useful than the adventurers around here.<end>"
KO: "마침 잘됐네. 지금 나 혼자야. 같이 할래? 내 입으로 말하긴 그렇지만 웬만한 모험자보다 훨씬 도움이 될 거야.<end>"

## 2600007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。助かる。<line-break>よろしくね。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. That'll be a big help. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 도움이 됐어. 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 2600008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec260-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　賢者の森にある猫屋敷？<line-break>…わかったわ。どこに居ても<line-break>そこで呼び出してくれるのね。<line-break>じゃ、待ってるから、<line-break>いつでも呼んでちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "What? Cat Manor in Sage's Forest? All right. So you'll summon me there no matter where I am. I'll be waiting, then. Call me whenever you like.<end>"
KO: "응? 현자의 숲에 있는 고양이 저택? …알았어. 어디 있든 거기서 불러 주는 거지. 그럼 기다릴 테니 언제든 불러 줘.<end>"

## 2610001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec261-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "男を捜している？<line-break>どんな野郎だ？<end>"
EN: "Looking for a man? What's he look like?<end>"
KO: "남자를 찾고 있다고? 어떤 놈인데?<end>"

## 2610002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec261-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "髪を頭の後ろに束ねていて、<line-break>顔には傷があるの。<line-break>こう、ほほの所に…。<end>"
EN: "His hair is tied back, and he has a scar on his face. Here, on his cheek...<end>"
KO: "머리를 뒤로 묶고 있고, 얼굴에는 상처가 있어. 이렇게, 뺨 쪽에….<end>"

## 2610003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec261-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷ねえ。<line-break>悪いがこの辺じゃ見かけねえな。<end>"
EN: "A scar on his cheek, eh? Sorry, haven't seen anyone like that around here.<end>"
KO: "뺨의 흉터라. 미안하지만 이 근처에선 본 적 없네.<end>"

## 2610004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec261-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…。邪魔したわね。<line-break>ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I see... Sorry to bother you. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 방해했네. 고마워.<end>"

## 2610005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec261-part99.toml

## 2620001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういえば、イーシャが<line-break>この間、ほほに傷のある男のことを<line-break>人に訊いていたみたいだけど…。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, Iria was asking around about a man with a scar on his cheek the other day.<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 이샤가 얼마 전에 뺨에 흉터가 있는 남자에 대해 사람들에게 물어보고 다닌 것 같은데….<end>"

## 2620003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天空神の神官服を着たおじさんだよ。<line-break>上門広場の階段に座ってらっしゃるのを<line-break>よく見かけるよ。<line-break>今は神官長を務める偉い方だけど<line-break>昔は、傭兵として鳴らしたらしいよ。<line-break>かなり実力のある人みたいだし。<end>"
EN: "He's an older man in the robes of a priest of the Sky God. I often see him sitting on the steps in Upper Gate Plaza. He's an important man and serves as chief priest now, but I hear he made a name for himself as a mercenary in the old days. He's apparently very skilled, too.<end>"
KO: "천공신 신관복을 입은 아저씨야. 상문 광장 계단에 앉아 계신 걸 자주 봐. 지금은 신관장인 높은 분인데 예전엔 용병으로 이름을 날렸대. 꽤 실력 있는 사람인가 봐.<end>"

## 2620004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度、アルノートゥンに行ったら<line-break>イオンズさんに尋ねてみようよ。<end>"
EN: "Next time we go to Alnortun, let's ask Ions.<end>"
KO: "다음에 알노툰에 가면 이온즈 씨에게 물어보자.<end>"

## 2620005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、イーシャさんが、<line-break>ほほにキズのある男の人のことを<line-break>たずねてまわってたんだ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so Iria was going around asking about a man with a scar on his cheek.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 이샤 씨가 볼에 상처가 있는 남자를 찾아다니고 있었구나….<end>"

## 2620006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、<line-break>その人かどうかわかんないけど、<line-break>アルノートゥンのイオンズさんかなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... I don't know if that's the person, but maybe it's Ions of Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "음, 그 사람인지는 모르겠지만 알노툰의 이온즈 씨 아닐까.<end>"

## 2620007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく、しょうこうきを上がったひろばの<line-break>階段にすわってるやさしそうな人だよ。<line-break>えらーい神官さんなんだって。<end>"
EN: "He's a kind-looking man who's often sitting on the steps in the plaza at the top of the lift. I hear he's a very important priest.<end>"
KO: "승강기를 올라간 광장 계단에 자주 앉아 있는 상냥해 보이는 사람이야. 아주 높은 신관님이래.<end>"

## 2620008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんどアルノートゥンに<line-break>行くことがあったら、一度、<line-break>たしかめてみるといいかも。<end>"
EN: "If you ever go to Alnortun, maybe you should check.<end>"
KO: "다음에 알노툰에 갈 일이 있으면 한번 확인해 보는 것도 좋겠네.<end>"

## 2620009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ？　なにを言い出すかと思えば<line-break>頬に傷のある下等生物に<line-break>心当たりはないか、ですってえ？<end>"
EN: "Huh? I was wondering what you were going to come out with. You want to know if I know of some lowly creature with a scar on its cheek?<end>"
KO: "하아? 무슨 소릴 하나 했더니 뺨에 흉터 있는 하등생물에 대해 아는 거 없냐고오?<end>"

## 2620010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴で優雅なエルフであるアタクシが<line-break>下等生物の顔なんて<line-break>いちいち覚えてるわけないじゃない！<line-break>どぉーしても知りたかったら<line-break>アルノートゥンの神官にでも頼んで、<line-break>天空神の神託でももらってくることね！<end>"
EN: "Why would a noble, graceful Elf such as myself bother remembering the faces of lowly creatures? If you simply must know, go ask one of the priests in Alnortun to obtain an oracle from the Sky God!<end>"
KO: "고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 이 몸이 하등생물 얼굴을 하나하나 기억할 리 없잖아! 그렇게나 알고 싶으면 알노툰 신관한테 부탁해서 천공신의 신탁이라도 받아 오라고!<end>"

## 2620011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…え？<line-break>アルノートゥンのイオ…ン…ズ…神官？<line-break>…の頬に傷があったのを思い出した…？<line-break>そ、そうよ！　当然よ！　もちろんよ！<line-break>そのイオ…ええと…もう誰でもいいわ！<line-break>…を思い出させるためそう言ったのよ！<end>"
EN: "What? Priest Io... n... s... of Alnortun? You just remembered the scar on that priest's cheek? Y-yes! Naturally! Of course! I said it to remind you of Io... um... whoever!<end>"
KO: "…뭐? 알노툰의 이오…ㄴ…즈… 신관? …뺨에 상처가 있었던 게 떠올랐어…? 그, 그래! 당연하지! 물론이야! 그 이오… 어쨌든 누구든 상관없어! …를 떠올리게 하려고 그런 말을 한 거야!<end>"

## 2620012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec262-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ド下等生物に何か教えるのも一苦労ね！<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>アタクシにお礼を言ってもよくってよ！<end>"
EN: "Teaching an utterly inferior creature anything is such an ordeal! <value:$28>! You may thank me now!<end>"
KO: "하등생물한테 뭘 가르치는 것도 힘드네! <value:$28>! 이 몸한테 감사하다고 해도 좋아!<end>"

## 2630001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、お主の仲間の女性が<line-break>ほほに傷のある男を捜しておって<line-break>それが、わしではないかと？<end>"
EN: "Hmm. A woman among your companions is looking for a man with a scar on his cheek, and you think that man might be me?<end>"
KO: "흠, 네 동료 여성이 볼에 상처가 있는 남자를 찾고 있고 그게 내가 아닐까 싶다는 건가?<end>"

## 2630002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わーはっはっはっ。<line-break>いくらなんでもそれは<line-break>人違いじゃろう。<line-break>確かにわしの顔にも傷はあるが、<line-break>女性に捜してもらえるほどの<line-break>色男ではないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Wah-ha-ha-ha! Surely you have the wrong man. True, I have a scar on my face, but I am hardly handsome enough to have a woman searching for me.<end>"
KO: "와하하하. 아무리 그래도 사람을 잘못 본 거겠지. 내 얼굴에도 상처는 있지만 여자가 찾아다닐 만큼 미남은 아니야.<end>"

## 2630003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほに傷のある男なら、<line-break>他にひとり知っておる。<line-break>興味があるのなら案内して進ぜよう。<end>"
EN: "I do know one other man with a scar on his cheek. If you are interested, I shall take you to him.<end>"
KO: "뺨에 상처가 있는 남자라면 다른 한 명도 알고 있네. 관심이 있다면 안내해 주지.<end>"

## 2630004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このクリスタルに入っている男の名は<line-break>ジリオン…。お主と同じく、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主じゃった。<line-break>かつてロストールの王権をめぐる<line-break>テジャワの変という争乱があってな。<line-break>ジリオンはそれに巻きこまれたのよ。<end>"
EN: "The man within this crystal is named Zillion. Like you, he possessed an Infinite Soul. Long ago, there was a conflict over the throne of Rostorl known as the Tejava Incident. Zillion was caught up in it.<end>"
KO: "이 크리스털 안에 있는 남자의 이름은 질리온…. 그대와 마찬가지로 무한의 소울을 지닌 자였지. 과거 로스톨의 왕권을 둘러싸고 테자와의 변이라는 분쟁이 있었고, 질리온은 거기에 휘말렸네.<end>"

## 2630005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当時、ジリオンと親しかった女の子…。<line-break>イーシャという娘がおってな。<line-break>　<line-break>彼女はテジャワの変の果てに<line-break>悪人の手でソウルを切り離されたのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>無論、ソウルを失えばその娘は消滅じゃ。<line-break>そこでジリオンは決断を下した。<line-break>　<line-break>自分のソウルと闇の神器・同化の髪飾りの<line-break>力を使ってイーシャの想いを支え、<line-break>彼女の命を存続させることを、な。<line-break>同化の髪飾りは確かにどんな欲望も<line-break>かなえるが、その欲望が<line-break>満たされることは決してない…。<end>"
EN: "At the time, there was a girl named Iria who was close to Zillion. By the end of the Tejava Incident, an evildoer had severed her Soul from her. Without her Soul, of course, Iria would cease to exist. So Zillion made his choice: he would use his own Soul and the power of the Dark Artifact known as the Hair Ornament of Assimilation to sustain Iria's inner life and keep her alive. The Hair Ornament of Assimilation can grant any desire, but it can never satisfy it.<end>"
KO: "당시 질리온과 가까웠던 여자아이…. 이샤라는 아이가 있었지. 그녀는 테자와의 변 끝에 악인의 손으로 소울이 분리되고 말았다. 물론 소울을 잃으면 그 아이는 소멸한다. 그래서 질리온은 결단을 내렸다. 자신의 소울과 어둠의 신기·동화의 머리장식 힘으로 이샤의 마음을 지탱해 그녀의 생명을 이어 가게 하기로. 동화의 머리장식은 어떤 욕망이든 이루지만 그 욕망은 결코 충족되지 않는다….<end>"

## 2630006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かくて、ジリオンの<line-break>イーシャを救いたいという願いは<line-break>かなった。<line-break>じゃが、ジリオン自身は、こうして<line-break>イーシャと言葉を交わすこともできず、<line-break>クリスタルの中で眠り続けておる。<line-break>まったく因果な宿命よ。<line-break>でも、それがジリオンの望んだ道<line-break>だったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "And so Zillion's wish to save Iria was granted. Yet Zillion himself cannot even speak with her. He remains asleep within the crystal, just as you see him now. A cruel fate indeed. But it was the path Zillion chose.<end>"
KO: "그리하여 이샤를 구하고 싶다는 질리온의 소원은 이루어졌다. 하지만 질리온 자신은 이렇게 이샤와 말 한마디 나누지 못한 채 크리스털 속에서 계속 잠들어 있지. 참으로 기구한 운명이야. 그래도 그것이 질리온이 원한 길이었네.<end>"

## 2630007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、今の話はジリオンから<line-break>固く口止めされておるから、<line-break>くれぐれも内密にな。<line-break>お主は信頼のおける人間だと<line-break>思ったからこそ教えたんじゃ。<line-break><line-break>さあ、長居は無用じゃ。行こうか。<end>"
EN: "Oh! Zillion told me in no uncertain terms not to tell anyone what I just told you, so keep it strictly between us. I only told you because I thought you could be trusted. Now, there's no point lingering here. Shall we go?<end>"
KO: "어이쿠, 방금 이야기는 질리온에게 단단히 입막음당했으니 절대 비밀로 해두게. 자네라면 믿을 만한 사람이라 생각해서 알려준 게야. 자, 오래 머물 필요 없지. 가볼까.<end>"

## 2630008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec263-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベント04用開終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질리온 & 이샤 이벤트04용 시작/끝<end>"

## 2640000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベント05用開始<end>"
EN: ""

## 2640001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あたしが冒険している理由？<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know why I'm an adventurer?<end>"
KO: "응? 내가 모험하는 이유?<end>"

## 2640002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。一緒に旅をしているんだし<line-break>それぐらいは話しておくべきかしら。<line-break>　<line-break>人を捜しているの。<end>"
EN: "Well, we are traveling together. I suppose I should tell you that much. I'm looking for someone.<end>"
KO: "그래. 함께 여행하는 사이니까 그 정도는 말해 둬야겠네. 사람을 찾고 있어.<end>"

## 2640003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、どこにいるのか今のところ<line-break>見当もつかないわ…。<end>"
EN: "I have no idea where he is yet.<end>"
KO: "글쎄, 지금은 어디 있는지 짐작도 안 가….<end>"

## 2640004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>今は詳しく話せない。<line-break>　<line-break>昔、いろいろあって、<line-break>すぐには他人を<line-break>信じることができないの…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I can't tell you the whole story yet. A lot happened in the past, and I can't bring myself to trust people so easily.<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 지금은 자세히 말할 수 없어요. 예전에 여러 일이 있어서 쉽게 다른 사람을 믿지 못해요….<end>"

## 2640005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気を悪くしないでね。<line-break>あたしに染みついた<line-break>習性みたいなものだから。<end>"
EN: "Please don't take it personally. It's just a habit I can't seem to shake.<end>"
KO: "기분 나빠하지 마. 내 몸에 밴 습관 같은 거니까.<end>"

## 2640006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと夜風に当たって<line-break>散歩してくるわ。おやすみ。<end>"
EN: "I'm going for a walk to get some night air. Good night.<end>"
KO: "잠깐 밤바람 쐬면서 산책하고 올게. 잘 자.<end>"

## 2640008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっと夜は独りで眠っていたから<line-break>パーティと一緒にいるのが<line-break>慣れなくって…。<line-break>…でも、誰かがずっとそばに<line-break>ついていてくれるのって、<line-break>そんなに悪い気分じゃないわね。<end>"
EN: "I've always slept alone at night, so I'm not used to having the party around. But having someone stay by my side all the time... it isn't such a bad feeling.<end>"
KO: "밤에는 늘 혼자 자 왔으니까 파티와 함께 있는 게 익숙하지 않아서…. …하지만 누군가가 계속 곁에 있어 주는 것도 그렇게 나쁜 기분은 아니네.<end>"

## 2640010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。褒めてくれて。<line-break>よく働くでしょ、あたし。<line-break>　<line-break>結構、見かけよりも年季が<line-break>入っているんだから。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. It's nice of you to say so. I work hard, don't I? I've got a lot more experience than you might think from looking at me.<end>"
KO: "고마워, 칭찬해 줘서. 나 꽤 열심히 일하지? 보기보다 경력도 제법 오래됐거든.<end>"

## 2640011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダムの宿屋で<line-break>働いていたこともあったのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もしかすると、その時に<line-break><value:$28>とも<line-break>会っていたかもね。ふふっ。<end>"
EN: "I once worked at an inn in Liberdam. Who knows? I might even have met you back then, <value:$28>. Heh.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤의 여관에서 일했던 적도 있어. 어쩌면 그때 <value:$28>과 만났을지도 모르겠네. 후훗.<end>"

## 2640013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あたしの生まれ？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…ロストールよ。<line-break>家はそこの下級貴族だったわ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、故郷にはいい思い出が<line-break>あまりないから話したくないの。<line-break>それ以上は訊かないで…。<end>"
EN: "What? Where was I born? ...Rostorl. My family belonged to the lesser nobility there. But I don't have many fond memories of home, so I'd rather not talk about it. Please don't ask me anything more...<end>"
KO: "응? 내 출신? …로스톨이야. 집안은 그곳의 하급 귀족이었어. 하지만 고향엔 좋은 추억이 별로 없어서 말하고 싶지 않아. 그 이상은 묻지 마….<end>"

## 2640015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あたしの捜している人が<line-break>どんな人かって？<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know what the person I'm looking for is like?<end>"
KO: "응? 내가 찾는 사람이 어떤 사람이냐고?<end>"

## 2640016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。<value:$28>がだいたい<line-break>どんな人かもわかってきたし。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしが捜している人の名前は<line-break>ジリオン…。<end>"
EN: "Well, I think I have a fair idea of what kind of person you are now, <value:$28>. The man I'm looking for is named Zillion...<end>"
KO: "그래. <value:$28>이 대충 어떤 사람인지도 알게 됐고. 내가 찾는 사람 이름은 질리온….<end>"

## 2640017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん。<line-break>あちこちで人に訊いているけど<line-break>まだ、これといった手がかりは…。<line-break>でも、どこにいようと<line-break>あいつがあたしのために<line-break>何かをしてるってことは感じるの。<line-break>たぶん、あいつががんばってるから<line-break>あたしがこうしていられるって…。<line-break>　<line-break>こんな感覚、うまく説明できないから、<line-break>話しても笑われるだけだと<line-break>思うけど…。<end>"
EN: "No. I've asked around everywhere, but I still haven't found any real leads... Even so, wherever he is, I can feel that he's doing something for me. Maybe it's because he's doing all he can that I'm still here like this... I can't explain the feeling very well. You'd probably only laugh if I told you...<end>"
KO: "아니. 여기저기 사람들에게 물어보고 있지만 아직 이렇다 할 단서는 없어…. 그래도 어디에 있든 그 녀석이 나를 위해 뭔가 하고 있다는 건 느껴져. 아마 그 녀석이 애쓰고 있으니까 내가 이렇게 있을 수 있는 것 같아…. 이런 감각은 잘 설명할 수 없어서 말해 봐야 웃음만 살 것 같지만….<end>"

## 2640018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。信じてくれるのね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あいつってとても強情だから<line-break>あたしに黙って何かに<line-break>耐えているんじゃないかなって。<line-break>そんな気がするの…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>だから絶対に見つけてやるのよ。<line-break>いつまでも恩着せられっぱなしなんて、<line-break>あたしの性に合わないし。<line-break>もし、あたしのために<line-break>何か無茶しているんだったら<line-break>必ず見つけて一発ひっぱたいてやるわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. You believe me. He's terribly stubborn, so I can't help thinking he may be enduring something without telling me. That's the feeling I get... And that's why I'm going to find him, no matter what. Staying in his debt forever just isn't in my nature. If he's doing something reckless for my sake, I'll find him and give him a good slap.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 믿어 주는구나. 그 녀석은 워낙 고집이 세서 나한테 말도 안 하고 뭔가를 참고 있는 게 아닐까 싶어. 그런 느낌이 들어…. 그러니까 반드시 찾아낼 거야. 언제까지나 신세만 지고 있는 건 내 성미에 안 맞거든. 만약 나 때문에 무모한 짓을 하고 있다면 반드시 찾아내서 한 대 갈겨 줄 거야.<end>"

## 2640019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話したら気持ちが少し落ち着いたわ。<line-break>ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I feel a little calmer now that I've talked about it. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "이야기하고 나니 마음이 조금 진정됐어. 고마워.<end>"

## 2640020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやすみなさい、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Good night, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "잘 자요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2640022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あたしが捜している<line-break>ジリオンのこと？<line-break>そうね。何から話していいのか…。<end>"
EN: "What? You mean Zillion, the man I'm looking for? Well... where should I begin?<end>"
KO: "응? 내가 찾고 있는 질리온 말이야? 그래. 어디부터 이야기해야 할까…<end>"

## 2640023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ駆け出しの冒険者だったころ<line-break>ジリオンはお父さんの形見だっていう<line-break>ショートソードを差しててね。<line-break>依頼をこなすのに躍起になってたわ。<line-break>初々しかったな。あのころはまだ<line-break>十代の後半ぐらいだったし。<line-break>たぶん今はもういい歳に<line-break>なってると思うけど…。<end>"
EN: "When Zillion was still a novice adventurer, he carried a short sword that he said was a keepsake from his father. He was desperate to complete every job he took. He was so fresh-faced back then, still only in his late teens. I imagine he must be well into adulthood by now...<end>"
KO: "아직 풋내기 모험가였을 때 질리온은 아버지의 유품이라는 쇼트소드 하나를 차고 의뢰를 해내는 데 혈안이었지. 참 풋풋했어. 그때는 아직 십 대 후반쯤이었고. 아마 지금은 제법 나이가 들었겠지만….<end>"

## 2640025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔を思い出していたの。<line-break>ロストールで起こった<line-break>テジャワの変っていう政変のこと。<line-break>ファーロス家の失権を狙う貴族たちが<line-break>起こした醜い争いよ。<line-break>多くの血が流されたわ。<line-break>そして、あたしとジリオンの運命も<line-break>あれをきっかけにして<line-break>一変してしまった…。<line-break>もう今さら、<line-break>どうしようもないことだけどね…。<end>"
EN: "I was remembering the past―the political upheaval in Rostorl known as the Tejava Incident. It was an ugly struggle started by nobles who sought to strip House Pharos of its power. A great deal of blood was shed, and it changed Zillion's fate and mine completely... Not that anything can be done about it now...<end>"
KO: "옛날 일을 떠올리고 있었어. 로스톨에서 일어난 테자와의 변이라는 정변 말이야. 파로스 가문의 실각을 노린 귀족들이 일으킨 추악한 싸움이었지. 많은 피가 흘렀어. 그리고 나와 질리온의 운명도 그 일을 계기로 완전히 바뀌어 버렸고…. 이제 와서는 어쩔 수 없는 일이지만….<end>"

## 2640027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまでずっと独りで生きてきたのに<line-break>今は<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>旅をしている…。<line-break>わからないものね、人の運命なんて。<line-break>でも、わくわくして楽しいわ。<line-break>こんな気持ち久しぶりよ。<end>"
EN: "I spent all those years living alone, and now I'm traveling with you, <value:$28>. You never can tell where fate will take you. But it's exciting. I'm having fun. I haven't felt this way in a long time.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 줄곧 혼자 살아왔는데 지금은 <value:$28>와 함께 여행하고 있어…. 사람의 운명이란 정말 모르는 거네. 그래도 두근거리고 즐거워. 이런 기분은 오랜만이야.<end>"

## 2640029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん、違うの。<line-break>ちょっと考え事をしていたら<line-break>気弱になっちゃって…。<end>"
EN: "No, that's not it. I got lost in thought and let myself lose heart...<end>"
KO: "아니, 그런 게 아니야. 잠깐 생각에 잠겼다가마음이 약해졌어….<end>"

## 2640030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫よ。ありがとう。<line-break>不思議ね。あなたの瞳を見ていると<line-break>なぜだか心が落ち着くわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right. Thank you. It's strange. Somehow, looking into your eyes puts me at ease.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 고마워. 이상하네. 네 눈을 보고 있으면 왠지 마음이 편안해져.<end>"

## 2640031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、ジリオンのこと？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そうね。どんな関係って<line-break>訊かれても…。<end>"
EN: "What? You mean Zillion? Well... I'm not sure how to describe our relationship.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 질리온 말이야? 글쎄. 무슨 관계냐고 물어도….<end>"

## 2640032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵だったり、味方だったり…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>協力したかと思ったら、<line-break>お互い意固地になって反発して…。<line-break>よくケンカしたわ。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes we were enemies, sometimes allies. One moment we'd be working together, and the next we'd both dig in our heels and turn against each other. We fought a lot.<end>"
KO: "적이었다가 아군이었다가…. 협력했나 싶으면 서로 고집부리며 맞서고…. 자주 싸웠지.<end>"

## 2640033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオンはぶっきらぼうだけど<line-break>子供には好かれていてね。<line-break>　<line-break>信じないと思うけど、<line-break>小さかったティアナ王女に<line-break>なつかれたこともあったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Zillion could be gruff, but children liked him. You may not believe it, but even Princess Tiana became attached to him when she was little.<end>"
KO: "질리온은 무뚝뚝하지만 아이들에게는 인기가 있었어. 믿기 어렵겠지만 어릴 적 티아나 공주가 따르던 적도 있었지.<end>"

## 2640034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、<value:$28>はどこか<line-break>あいつに似ているわ。<line-break>　<line-break>優しくて強くて、<line-break>他人のために一生懸命で…。<line-break>　<line-break>昔のあたしはずっと独りで生きてきて<line-break>失うものがないからこそ<line-break>自分は強いのだと無邪気に信じていた。<line-break>でも、ジリオンやあなたと出会って、<line-break>あたしは初めて、人のために<line-break>強くなれることを知った…。<end>"
EN: "But there's something about you that reminds me of him, <value:$28>. You're kind and strong, and you work so hard for other people... I used to live entirely on my own. I naively believed I was strong because I had nothing to lose. But after meeting Zillion and you, I learned for the first time that I could become strong for someone else's sake...<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>는 어딘가 그 녀석을 닮았어. 다정하고 강하고, 남을 위해서라면 정말 열심이고…. 예전의 나는 늘 혼자 살아왔고 잃을 게 없기 때문에 내가 강한 거라고 순진하게 믿었어. 하지만 질리온과 너를 만나고 처음으로, 사람은 누군가를 위해 강해질 수 있다는 걸 알게 됐어….<end>"

## 2640035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>に<line-break>出会えて良かった。<line-break>　<line-break>いつの日か、あたしが<line-break>おばあちゃんになったら、<line-break>　<line-break>あなたとの冒険を孫たちに自慢して<line-break>聞かせるわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad I met you, <value:$28>. Someday, when I'm a grandmother, I'll boast to my grandchildren about our adventures together.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>을 만나서 다행이야. 언젠가 내가 할머니가 되면 너와 함께한 모험을 손주들에게 자랑하며 들려줄게.<end>"

## 2640036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっとも、そんな未来があればの<line-break>話だけど…。<end>"
EN: "Assuming that future ever comes, of course...<end>"
KO: "물론 그런 미래가 있다면 말이지만….<end>"

## 2640037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。あたしが落ちこんでいたら<line-break>それこそ救いようがないわね。<line-break>　<line-break>ありがとう。<value:$28>。<line-break>少し元気が出てきたわ。<line-break>もう大丈夫よ。<end>"
EN: "You're right. If I let myself get down, then I really would be beyond help. Thank you, <value:$28>. I'm feeling a little better now. I'll be all right.<end>"
KO: "그래. 내가 풀이 죽어 있으면 정말 답이 없겠지. 고마워, <value:$28>. 조금 기운이 났어. 이제 괜찮아.<end>"

## 2640039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちに来ない？　いい星空よ。<end>"
EN: "Why don't you come over here? The stars are beautiful.<end>"
KO: "이쪽으로 올래? 별이 정말 예뻐.<end>"

## 2640040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界がどんなに変わっても、<line-break>この星空は変わらない…。<line-break>　<line-break>人の心もこの星空のように<line-break>永遠に変わらなければいいのに…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、そう望んでしまうことこそが<line-break>人の心の弱さなのかもね…。<end>"
EN: "No matter how much the world changes, these stars remain the same... I wish people's hearts could remain forever unchanged, just like them... But perhaps wishing for that is itself a weakness of the human heart...<end>"
KO: "세상이 아무리 변해도 이 별하늘은 변하지 않아…. 사람의 마음도 이 별하늘처럼 영원히 변하지 않으면 좋을 텐데…. 하지만 그렇게 바라 버리는 것 자체가 인간 마음의 약함일지도 모르겠네….<end>"

## 2640042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、ジリオンのことを？<end>"
EN: "What, you want to hear about Zillion?<end>"
KO: "어, 질리온 말이야?<end>"

## 2640044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "可哀相なアトレイア様のこと思うと<line-break>今でも胸が痛むの…。<end>"
EN: "My heart still aches whenever I think of poor Lady Atleia...<end>"
KO: "불쌍한 아트레이아 님을 생각하면 지금도 가슴이 아파….<end>"

## 2640047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今なら<value:$28>に話せるかな。<line-break>あたし昔、王宮でエリス様に<line-break>仕えていたころがあったの…。<line-break>これでも腕利きのつもりでいたんだけど<line-break>結局はただ政争の道具として<line-break>操られているだけだった。<line-break>そんなあたしに人としての生き方を<line-break>思い出させてくれたのが<line-break>ジリオンよ…。<line-break>彼がいなければ、たぶん<line-break>あたしもここにはいなかった。<line-break>いつもそう思っているわ。<end>"
EN: "I think I can tell you now, <value:$28>. I once served Lady Eris at the royal palace. I fancied myself quite capable, but in the end, I was only being manipulated as a tool in a political struggle. Zillion was the one who reminded me how to live as a human being. If it weren't for him, I probably wouldn't be here now. I've always believed that.<end>"
KO: "지금이라면 <value:$28>에게 말할 수 있을까. 나 예전에 왕궁에서 에리스 님을 모시던 적이 있었어…. 나름대로 실력자라고 생각했지만 결국 정쟁의 도구로 휘둘릴 뿐이었지. 그런 나에게 인간답게 사는 법을 되찾게 해 준 사람이 질리온이야…. 그가 없었다면 아마 나도 여기 없었을 거야. 늘 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 2640049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちに来ない？　いい星空よ。<end>"
EN: "Why don't you come over here? The stars are beautiful.<end>"
KO: "이쪽으로 올래? 별이 정말 예뻐.<end>"

## 2640050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界がどんなに変わっても、<line-break>この星空は変わらない…。<line-break>　<line-break>人の心もこの星空のように<line-break>永遠に変わらなければいいのに…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、そう望んでしまうことこそが<line-break>人の心の弱さなのかもね…。<end>"
EN: "No matter how much the world changes, these stars remain the same... I wish people's hearts could remain forever unchanged, just like them... But perhaps wishing for that is itself a weakness of the human heart...<end>"
KO: "세상이 아무리 변해도 이 별하늘은 변하지 않아…. 사람의 마음도 이 별하늘처럼 영원히 변하지 않으면 좋을 텐데…. 하지만 그렇게 바라 버리는 것 자체가 인간 마음의 약함일지도 모르겠네….<end>"

## 2640051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>最近、あたし、思うようになったの。<line-break>　<line-break>これ以上、多くを望んでしまう前に、<line-break>今、このまま消えることになったら<line-break>それが一番幸せなのかもしれないって。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I've been thinking lately. Before I begin to wish for anything more, perhaps the greatest happiness would be to simply disappear now, while everything is still like this.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 요즘 나, 이런 생각을 하게 됐어. 더 많은 걸 바라기 전에 지금 이대로 사라질 수 있다면 그게 가장 행복할지도 모른다고.<end>"

## 2640052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは。バカね。<line-break>消えやしないわよ。<line-break>そのくらい、幸せだなって思ったの。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha. Don't be silly. I'm not going to disappear. I only meant that this is how happy I feel.<end>"
KO: "아하하. 바보네. 사라지지 않아. 그만큼 행복하다고 생각한 거야.<end>"

## 2640053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "覚えておくといいわ、<line-break>鈍感なる無限のソウルの持ち主さん。<line-break>　<line-break>女の子は<line-break>幸せの絶頂にも<line-break>不安を感じてしまうんだって。<end>"
EN: "Remember this, you obtuse bearer of the Infinite Soul: even at the height of happiness, a girl can still feel uneasy.<end>"
KO: "기억해 둬, 둔감한 무한의 소울을 가진 사람. 여자아이는 행복의 절정에서도 불안을 느끼는 법이야.<end>"

## 2640054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あと、こんな風に<line-break>話をした女の子がいたことも<line-break>覚えていてね。<end>"
EN: "And remember that there was a girl who spoke to you this way.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 이런 식으로 이야기했던 여자아이가 있었다는 것도 기억해 줘.<end>"

## 2640055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "胸が…苦しいの。<end>"
EN: "My chest... it hurts.<end>"
KO: "가슴이… 답답해.<end>"

## 2640056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>…知らない。<line-break>　<line-break>…ねえ、ひとつだけ訊いていい？<line-break>無限のソウルを授かると<line-break>人ってどうして鈍感になる…の？<end>"
EN: "...I don't know. Listen, may I ask you just one thing? Why does receiving the Infinite Soul make a person so... oblivious?<end>"
KO: "………………. …몰라. …있지, 하나만 물어봐도 돼? 무한의 소울을 받으면 사람은 왜 그렇게 둔감해지는 거야…?<end>"

## 2640057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめんなさい。<line-break>あたし…どうかしてる。<line-break>忘れて。<line-break>…おやすみ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I... don't know what's come over me. Forget it. Good night, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…미안해. 나… 이상한가 봐. 잊어 줘. …잘 자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2640058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あたしが冒険している理由？<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know why I'm an adventurer?<end>"
KO: "응? 내가 모험하는 이유?<end>"

## 2640059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。一緒に旅をしてるんだし<line-break>それぐらいは話しておくべきかしら。<line-break>　<line-break>人を捜しているの。<end>"
EN: "Well, we are traveling together. I suppose I should tell you that much. I'm looking for someone.<end>"
KO: "그래. 같이 여행 중이니 그 정도는 말해 둬야겠지. 사람을 찾고 있어.<end>"

## 2640060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、どこにいるのか今のところ<line-break>見当もつかないわ…。<end>"
EN: "I have no idea where he is yet.<end>"
KO: "글쎄, 지금은 어디 있는지 짐작도 안 가….<end>"

## 2640061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>今は詳しく話せない。<line-break>　<line-break>昔、いろいろあって、<line-break>すぐには他人を<line-break>信じることができないの…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I can't tell you the whole story yet. A lot happened in the past, and I can't bring myself to trust people so easily.<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 지금은 자세히 말할 수 없어요. 예전에 여러 일이 있어서 쉽게 다른 사람을 믿지 못해요….<end>"

## 2640062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気を悪くしないでね。<line-break>あたしに染みついた<line-break>習性みたいなものだから。<end>"
EN: "Please don't take it personally. It's just a habit I can't seem to shake.<end>"
KO: "기분 나빠하지 마. 내 몸에 밴 습관 같은 거니까.<end>"

## 2640063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと夜風に当たって<line-break>散歩してくるわ。おやすみ。<end>"
EN: "I'm going for a walk to get some night air. Good night.<end>"
KO: "잠깐 밤바람 쐬면서 산책하고 올게. 잘 자.<end>"

## 2640065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あたしの捜している人が<line-break>どんな人かって？<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know what the person I'm looking for is like?<end>"
KO: "응? 내가 찾는 사람이 어떤 사람이냐고?<end>"

## 2640066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。<value:$28>がだいたい<line-break>どんな人かもわかってきたし。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしが捜している人の名前は<line-break>ジリオン…。<end>"
EN: "Well, I think I have a fair idea of what kind of person you are now, <value:$28>. The man I'm looking for is named Zillion...<end>"
KO: "그래. <value:$28>이 대충 어떤 사람인지도 알게 됐고. 내가 찾는 사람 이름은 질리온….<end>"

## 2640067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん。<line-break>あちこちで人に訊いているけど<line-break>まだ、これといった手がかりは…。<line-break>でも、どこにいようと<line-break>あいつがあたしのために<line-break>何かをしてるってことは感じるの。<line-break>たぶん、あいつががんばってるから<line-break>あたしがこうしていられるって…。<line-break>　<line-break>こんな感覚、うまく説明できないから、<line-break>話しても笑われるだけだと<line-break>思うけど…。<end>"
EN: "No. I've asked around everywhere, but I still haven't found any real leads... Even so, wherever he is, I can feel that he's doing something for me. Maybe it's because he's doing all he can that I'm still here like this... I can't explain the feeling very well. You'd probably only laugh if I told you...<end>"
KO: "아니. 여기저기 사람들에게 물어보고 있지만 아직 이렇다 할 단서는 없어…. 그래도 어디에 있든 그 녀석이 나를 위해 뭔가 하고 있다는 건 느껴져. 아마 그 녀석이 애쓰고 있으니까 내가 이렇게 있을 수 있는 것 같아…. 이런 감각은 잘 설명할 수 없어서 말해 봐야 웃음만 살 것 같지만….<end>"

## 2640068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。信じてくれるのね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あいつってとても強情だから<line-break>あたしに黙って何かに<line-break>耐えているんじゃないかなって。<line-break>そんな気がするの…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>だから絶対に見つけてやるのよ。<line-break>いつまでも恩着せられっぱなしなんて、<line-break>あたしの性に合わないし。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. You believe me. He's terribly stubborn, so I can't help thinking he may be enduring something without telling me. That's the feeling I get... And that's why I'm going to find him, no matter what. Staying in his debt forever just isn't in my nature.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 믿어 주는구나. 그 녀석은 워낙 고집이 세니까 나한테 말도 안 하고 뭔가를 혼자 참고 있는 게 아닐까 싶어. 그런 느낌이 들어…. 그러니까 반드시 찾아낼 거야. 언제까지나 신세만 지고 있는 건 내 성미에 안 맞거든.<end>"

## 2640069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしが意地っ張り？<line-break>ジリオンにも同じことを言われたわ。<line-break>　<line-break>一度なんて、面と向かって馬鹿って<line-break>言われたのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ホント、どうしてこう<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主って<line-break>鈍感なのかしら。<end>"
EN: "You think I'm stubborn? Zillion said the same thing to me. Once, he even called me an idiot to my face. Honestly, why are bearers of the Infinite Soul always so obtuse?<end>"
KO: "내가 고집불통이라고? 질리온도 똑같은 말을 했어. 한 번은 면전에서 바보라고까지 했다니까. 정말이지 왜 무한의 소울을 가진 자들은 하나같이 이렇게 둔감한 걸까.<end>"

## 2640070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよ。ジリオンも<line-break><value:$28>と同じように<line-break>無限のソウルを持っていたわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Zillion possessed the Infinite Soul, just as you do, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래. 질리온도 <value:$28>처럼 무한한 소울을 가지고 있었어.<end>"

## 2640071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、たぶんあたしのために<line-break>その力を…。<end>"
EN: "But that power of his... perhaps, for my sake...<end>"
KO: "하지만 아마 나를 위해 그 힘을….<end>"

## 2640072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>励ましてくれて。<line-break>　<line-break>そうね。あたしが落ちこんでいても<line-break>仕方ないわよね。<line-break>　<line-break>今のあたしにできることは<line-break>一日でも早くジリオンを<line-break>捜しだすこと…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for encouraging me, <value:$28>. You're right. There's no point in letting myself get discouraged. All I can do now is find Zillion as soon as possible...<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 격려해 줘서. 그래. 내가 우울해해도 소용없겠지. 지금 내가 할 수 있는 건 하루라도 빨리 질리온을 찾아내는 것….<end>"

## 2640073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>に話したおかげで<line-break>少しは胸のつかえが取れたみたい。<line-break>　<line-break>お陰でまた明日からがんばってけそう。<line-break>ありがと、<value:$28>。<line-break>おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Talking to you has lifted some of the weight from my chest, <value:$28>. I think I can keep going again tomorrow. Thank you, <value:$28>. Good night.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>에게 이야기하고 나니 조금은 속이 후련해진 것 같아. 덕분에 내일부터 다시 힘낼 수 있을 것 같아. 고마워, <value:$28>. 잘 자.<end>"

## 2640075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>ちょっと考え事をしていたら<line-break>混乱しちゃって…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I was thinking about things and got myself all confused.<end>"
KO: "미안해. 잠깐 생각에 잠겼다가 혼란스러워졌어….<end>"

## 2640076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、あたしにもどうしたらいいか<line-break>わからないのよ…。<end>"
EN: "I don't know what to do anymore...<end>"
KO: "이젠 나도 어떻게 해야 할지 모르겠어….<end>"

## 2640077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時々、ジリオンの顔を<line-break>忘れそうになるの。<line-break>　<line-break>すぐに思い出せたあいつの笑顔が、<line-break>どんどん薄れていく。<line-break>それがどうしようもなく恐いの…。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes I feel as though I'm forgetting Zillion's face. I used to be able to picture his smile at once, but it's fading more and more. It terrifies me...<end>"
KO: "가끔 질리온의 얼굴을 잊어버릴 것 같아. 금방 떠올릴 수 있었던 그 녀석의 웃는 얼굴이 점점 희미해져. 그게 견딜 수 없이 무서워….<end>"

## 2640078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったい、あたしは今、<line-break>誰を想っているのか…。<line-break>　<line-break>それがわからなくて不安に<line-break>押しつぶされそうになる。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしの願いはただジリオンを<line-break>捜しだすことだけだった。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、今は本当にそれが望みなのか<line-break>自分でもわからない…。<end>"
EN: "Who am I thinking of now...? Not knowing makes me feel as though the anxiety will crush me. All I ever wanted was to find Zillion. But now I don't even know if that's truly what I want anymore...<end>"
KO: "대체 나는 지금 누구를 생각하고 있는 걸까…. 그걸 모르겠어서 불안에 짓눌릴 것 같아. 내 소원은 그저 질리온을 찾아내는 것뿐이었어. 하지만 지금은 정말 그게 내가 바라는 건지 나도 모르겠어….<end>"

## 2640079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。焦ってもしょうがないわよね。<line-break>とにかく今はジリオンを<line-break>捜しださないと。<line-break>でないと、あたしは自分の心に…。<end>"
EN: "You're right. There's no use rushing. For now, I simply have to find Zillion. Otherwise, my own heart...<end>"
KO: "그래. 조급해해 봐야 소용없지. 아무튼 지금은 질리온을 찾아야 해. 그러지 않으면 난 내 마음에….<end>"

## 2640080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん。なんでもないわ。<line-break>おやすみなさい、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "No, it's nothing. Good night, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아니, 아무것도 아니야. 잘 자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2640081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec264-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベント05用開終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질리온 & 이샤 이벤트05용 시작/끝<end>"

## 2650000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: ""
