## 2650001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見つけたよ。闇の神器のひとつ、<line-break>同化の髪飾り…。<line-break>やっぱり、そこにあったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Found it. One of the Dark Artifacts―the Hair Ornament of Assimilation. So it really was there.<end>"
KO: "찾았어. 어둠의 신기 중 하나, 동화의 머리장식…. 역시 거기 있었구나.<end>"

## 2650002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、誰？<line-break>同化の髪飾りがどこにあるのか<line-break>知ってるの？<end>"
EN: "Who are you? Do you know where the Hair Ornament of Assimilation is?<end>"
KO: "당신, 누구야? 동화의 머리장식이 어디 있는지 알아?<end>"

## 2650003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はシャリ。<line-break>君のことは知ってるよ、イーシャ。<line-break>自己紹介には及ばない。<line-break>さて、質問の続きだけど、<line-break>同化の髪飾りならそこさ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Shalli. I already know who you are, Iria, so there's no need for introductions. Now, to answer your question: the Hair Ornament of Assimilation is right there.<end>"
KO: "나는 샤리. 네가 누군지는 알고 있어, 이샤. 자기소개는 필요 없어. 자, 질문의 계속인데 동화의 머리장식이라면 저기야.<end>"

## 2650004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこだよ……君の核……。<line-break>そこにあるんだ。同化の髪飾りがね。<end>"
EN: "Right there... in your core. That's where the Hair Ornament of Assimilation is.<end>"
KO: "거기야…… 네 핵…… 거기에 있어. 동화의 머리장식이.<end>"

## 2650005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あたしの…？<end>"
EN: "I-In me...?<end>"
KO: "내, 내 거야…?<end>"

## 2650006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れているだろうけど、<line-break>君は一度、ソウルを失ったんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>魔道士マスカードの操る<line-break>魂切の鎌で、胸を貫かれてね。<line-break>　<line-break>君が今、こうして存在していられるのは<line-break>身代わりになってソウルを捧げた人が<line-break>いるからさ。<end>"
EN: "You probably don't remember, but you lost your Soul once. The sorcerer Mascar drove the Soul-Reaping Scythe through your chest. You can exist as you do now only because someone took your place and offered up his Soul.<end>"
KO: "잊고 있겠지만 너는 한 번 소울을 잃었어. 마도사 마스카드가 다루는 혼절의 낫에 가슴을 꿰뚫렸지. 네가 지금 이렇게 존재할 수 있는 건 대신 소울을 바친 사람이 있기 때문이야.<end>"

## 2650007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさかその人って…ジリオン！？<end>"
EN: "You don't mean... Zillion!?<end>"
KO: "설마 그 사람이… 질리온!?<end>"

## 2650008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハ。ご名答！<end>"
EN: "Hahahaha! Correct!<end>"
KO: "아하하하. 정답!<end>"

## 2650009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオンの無限のソウルと<line-break>同化の髪飾りの力で<line-break>今の君は生かされているのさ。<line-break>君の核を…君の想いで２つの力に、<line-break>支えさせているんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Zillion's Infinite Soul and the power of the Hair Ornament of Assimilation are what keep you alive now. Your feelings make those two powers sustain your core.<end>"
KO: "질리온의 무한의 소울과 동화의 머리장식 힘으로 지금의 넌 살아 있는 거야. 네 핵을… 네 마음으로 두 힘이 지탱하게 하고 있지.<end>"

## 2650010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…そんなことが……。<end>"
EN: "No... That can't be...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가… 그런 일이…….<end>"

## 2650011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぎれもない事実だよ。<line-break>君は認めたくないだろうけど。<line-break>　<line-break>…それに、君の想いはかなわない。<line-break>君が現れたとき、彼が消えた…。<line-break>今度は彼が現れれば、君が消える。<line-break>さて、どうすればいいのかな？<end>"
EN: "It's the undeniable truth, whether you want to accept it or not. And your wish can never come true. When you appeared, he vanished. If he returns, you'll be the one to vanish. So what can be done?<end>"
KO: "틀림없는 사실이야. 네가 인정하고 싶지 않겠지만. …그리고 네 마음은 이뤄지지 않아. 네가 나타났을 때 그가 사라졌어…. 이번엔 그가 나타나면 네가 사라질 거야. 자, 어떻게 해야 할까?<end>"

## 2650012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わからない。<line-break>あたしにはどうすればいいのか…。<end>"
EN: "I don't know. I don't know what I'm supposed to do...<end>"
KO: "…모르겠어. 난 어떻게 해야 할지….<end>"

## 2650013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "簡単だよ。不公平を解消すればいいのさ。<line-break>君自身は消えてしまうけど、それで<line-break>どちらの願いもかなうことになる。<line-break>肉体を捨てることで、君とジリオンは<line-break>永遠に一緒にいられるんだよ。<end>"
EN: "It's simple. All you have to do is set things right. You'll disappear, but both wishes will come true. Once you cast off your body, you and Zillion can be together forever.<end>"
KO: "간단해. 불공평을 없애면 돼. 너 자신은 사라지지만 그러면 양쪽의 소원이 모두 이루어져. 육체를 버리면 너와 질리온은 영원히 함께 있을 수 있어.<end>"

## 2650014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとは君の決断ひとつだ。<line-break>君は消えて願いをかなえ、<line-break>僕はその報酬に同化の髪飾りをもらう。<line-break>悪くない取引だよ。<line-break>さあ、おいで。イーシャ。<end>"
EN: "All that's left is for you to decide. You disappear and get your wish, and I receive the Hair Ornament of Assimilation as my reward. Not a bad bargain. Come along, Iria.<end>"
KO: "이제 네 결단 하나뿐이야. 넌 사라져서 소원을 이루고, 난 보상으로 동화의 머리장식을 받는다. 나쁘지 않은 거래야. 자, 이리 와. 이샤.<end>"

## 2650015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けに入る<end>"
EN: "Step in to help<end>"
KO: "도우러 나선다<end>"

## 2650016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "様子を見る<end>"
EN: "Wait and see<end>"
KO: "상황을 지켜본다<end>"

## 2650018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無粋な邪魔が入っちゃったね。<line-break>僕の力が必要になったときは<line-break>ゼグナ鉱山に来るといいよ。イーシャ。<end>"
EN: "What an uncouth interruption. If you ever find yourself in need of my power, come to Zegna Mine, Iria.<end>"
KO: "눈치 없는 방해가 끼어들었네. 내 힘이 필요해지면 제그나 광산으로 오면 돼, 이샤.<end>"

## 2650019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君も鈍感だなぁ。<line-break>素人はこれだから困るんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>彼女は優しい子だったけど<line-break>幼いころから愛にめぐまれなかった。<line-break>　<line-break>それを利用されて陰謀に巻きこまれ、<line-break>挙げ句の果てに、魂切の鎌で<line-break>ソウルを刈り取られたのさ。<line-break>ソウルが消えた時点でその人は<line-break>もう終わりだよ。普通ならね。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、ジリオンの強い想いが、<line-break>奇跡的に彼女を救った。<line-break>　<line-break>欲望をかなえる闇の神器・同化の髪飾りと<line-break>ジリオンのソウルが、彼女の想いを支え、<line-break>その消滅を食い止めているんだ。<end>"
EN: "You're rather slow on the uptake yourself. Amateurs are always such a nuisance. She was a kind child, but she grew up starved of love. Someone exploited that and drew her into a conspiracy, and in the end the Soul-Reaping Scythe harvested her Soul. Once a person's Soul is gone, that's the end. Ordinarily, at least. But the strength of Zillion's feelings miraculously saved her. The Hair Ornament of Assimilation, a Dark Artifact that grants desires, and Zillion's Soul sustain her feelings and keep her from vanishing.<end>"
KO: "너도 참 둔하네. 초보는 이래서 곤란하다니까. 그녀는 다정한 아이였지만 어릴 때부터 사랑을 받지 못했어. 그 점을 이용당해 음모에 휘말렸고, 결국 혼절의 낫에 소울을 베였지. 소울이 사라지는 순간 그 사람은 끝이야. 보통이라면 말이지. 하지만 질리온의 강한 마음이 기적적으로 그녀를 구했어. 욕망을 이루는 어둠의 신기·동화의 머리장식과 질리온의 소울이 그녀의 마음을 지탱해 소멸을 막고 있는 거야.<end>"

## 2650020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の彼女を支えているのは<line-break>ジリオンへの強い想いだけだよ。<line-break>でも、その想いがいつまで続くかな？<line-break>人間の心なんて、はかなく、<line-break>移ろいやすい。<line-break>　<line-break>彼女の想いが弱まれば、<line-break>彼女自身も消えてしまうんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>僕はジリオンの居場所を知っている。<line-break>今ならまだイーシャの想いをそこに<line-break>運ぶことができたんだ。<end>"
EN: "The only thing sustaining her now is the strength of her feelings for Zillion. But how long will those feelings last? The human heart is frail and fickle. If her feelings weaken, she herself will vanish. I know where Zillion is. There was still time for me to carry Iria's feelings there.<end>"
KO: "지금 그녀를 지탱하는 건 질리온을 향한 강한 마음뿐이야. 하지만 그 마음이 언제까지 이어질까? 인간의 마음은 덧없고 쉽게 변하지. 그녀의 마음이 약해지면 그녀 자신도 사라지고 말아. 나는 질리온이 어디 있는지 알아. 지금이라면 아직 이샤의 마음을 그곳까지 가져갈 수 있었어.<end>"

## 2650021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが最良の結果になるはずだった…。<line-break>無知な君に台無しにされなければね。<line-break>ホントに善良なお節介焼きだよ、君は。<end>"
EN: "That should have been the best possible outcome... until your ignorance ruined it. You really are a well-meaning meddler.<end>"
KO: "그게 최선의 결과가 될 예정이었어…. 무지한 네가 망쳐 놓지만 않았더라면 말이지. 정말이지 선량한 참견쟁이야, 너는.<end>"

## 2650022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったわ…。<line-break>それでジリオンと一緒になれるのなら。<end>"
EN: "All right... If it means I can be with Zillion.<end>"
KO: "알겠어…. 그걸로 질리온과 함께할 수 있다면.<end>"

## 2650023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい子だ。イーシャ。<line-break>僕は約束を守るよ。<end>"
EN: "Good girl, Iria. I'll keep my promise.<end>"
KO: "착한 아이구나, 이샤. 나는 약속을 지킬게.<end>"

## 2650024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec265-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢明な判断だったね。<value:$28>。<line-break>君と僕が傷つけ合うこともなく<line-break>イーシャの願いはかなえられた。<line-break>同化の髪飾りは僕がもらっていくよ。<line-break>まさか異論はないよね？　アハハハ。<end>"
EN: "A wise decision, <value:$28>. Iria's wish was granted without you and me having to hurt each other. I'll be taking the Hair Ornament of Assimilation. Surely you don't object? Hahahaha.<end>"
KO: "현명한 판단이었어, <value:$28>. 너와 내가 서로 상처 입힐 일도 없이 이샤의 소원은 이루어졌지. 동화의 머리장식은 내가 가져갈게. 설마 이견은 없겠지? 아하하하.<end>"

## 2660001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン…。<line-break>あなたは今、どこにいるの…？<line-break>もう一度、あなたの声が聞きたい…。<end>"
EN: "Zillion... Where are you now? I want to hear your voice again...<end>"
KO: "질리온…. 당신은 지금 어디에 있어…? 다시 한번 당신 목소리를 듣고 싶어….<end>"

## 2660003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫よ。疲れてなんかいないわ。<line-break>ありがとう。心配してくれて。<line-break>ちょっと彼のことを思い出していたの。<end>"
EN: "I'm fine. I'm not tired at all. Thank you for worrying about me. I was just thinking about him.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 피곤하지 않아. 걱정해 줘서 고마워. 그냥 그 사람 생각을 좀 하고 있었어.<end>"

## 2660004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　ジリオンのことが聞きたいの？<line-break>べつに構わないけど…。<line-break>うーん、何から話していいのか…。<line-break>あたしと知り合ったころは<line-break>まだ駆け出しの冒険者で、<line-break>頑固で無鉄砲な命知らずだったわ。<end>"
EN: "What? You want to hear about Zillion? I don't mind... I'm just not sure where to start. When we first met, he was still a fledgling adventurer―stubborn, reckless, and with no regard for his own life.<end>"
KO: "응? 질리온 이야기를 듣고 싶어? 상관없긴 한데…. 음, 어디서부터 이야기해야 하지…. 나와 처음 알게 됐을 때는 아직 풋내기 모험가였고, 고집 세고 무모한 목숨 모르는 녀석이었어.<end>"

## 2660005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、いざという時には度胸があって、<line-break>彼の機転で助かったことも<line-break>何度かあったわね。<line-break>とても不器用だけど優しくて、<line-break>あいつがそばにいるだけでなぜか<line-break>あたしは安らぎを覚えたの。<end>"
EN: "But when it counted, he had courage, and his quick thinking proved a lifesaver more than once. He was terribly awkward, but kind. Somehow, just having him beside me put me at ease.<end>"
KO: "하지만 막상 일이 닥치면 배짱도 있고, 그 애의 재치 덕분에 도움받은 적도 몇 번 있었지. 정말 서툴지만 다정하고, 그 녀석이 곁에 있는 것만으로도 이상하게 마음이 편안해졌어.<end>"

## 2660006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　ジリオンにもう一度<line-break>会いたいかって？<line-break>　<line-break>もしかして、<value:$28>は<line-break>彼がどこにいるのか知ってるの？<end>"
EN: "What? Are you asking if I want to see Zillion again? Wait... <value:$28>, do you know where he is?<end>"
KO: "뭐? 질리온을 다시 만나고 싶냐고? 혹시 <value:$28>은 그가 어디 있는지 알아?<end>"

## 2660007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼のことを教える<end>"
EN: "Tell her about Zillion<end>"
KO: "그 사람에 대해 알려준다<end>"

## 2660008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙っている<end>"
EN: "Remain silent<end>"
KO: "가만히 있는다<end>"

## 2660009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオンが…アルノートゥンの<line-break>クリスタルの中に？<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあ、シャリの言っていたことは<line-break>本当だったのね…。<end>"
EN: "Zillion is... inside the crystal of Alnortun? Then what Shalli told me was true...<end>"
KO: "질리온이… 알노툰의 크리스탈 안에? 그럼 샤리가 말한 게 사실이었구나….<end>"

## 2660010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルを失ったあたしを助けるために<line-break>ジリオンが…。<line-break>バカよ…。ジリオン…。<line-break>どうして、あたしなんかのために…。<end>"
EN: "Zillion did that... to save me after I lost my Soul? You fool... Zillion... Why would you do that for someone like me...?<end>"
KO: "소울을 잃은 나를 구하려고 질리온이…. 바보야… 질리온…. 왜 나 같은 걸 위해서….<end>"

## 2660011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたとの旅もここでおしまい。<line-break>あたし、ゼグナ鉱山に行くわ。<line-break>シャリに会って、<line-break>すべてを終わらせなきゃ…。<line-break>さようなら。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. Our journey together ends here. I'm going to Zegna Mine. I have to see Shalli and put an end to all of this... Goodbye.<end>"
KO: "미안해, <value:$28>. 너와의 여행도 여기서 끝이야. 나, 제그나 광산에 갈게. 샤리를 만나 모든 걸 끝내야 해…. 안녕.<end>"

## 2660012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよね。<value:$28>がいくら<line-break>大陸中を旅してるといっても<line-break>ジリオンのことは知らないわよね。<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>もしかしてと思ったから…。<end>"
EN: "You're right. You've traveled all over the continent, <value:$28>, but you wouldn't know about Zillion. I'm sorry. I just thought you might...<end>"
KO: "그렇지. <value:$28>가 아무리 대륙 곳곳을 여행한다 해도 질리온까지는 모르겠지. 미안해. 혹시나 해서 물어본 거야…<end>"

## 2660013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec266-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "夜も更けてきたし<line-break>あたし、先に休ませてもらうね。<line-break>おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "It's getting late, so I think I'll turn in. Good night.<end>"
KO: "밤도 깊었으니 난 먼저 쉬러 갈게. 잘 자.<end>"

## 2670001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同化の髪飾りはあげるわ。<line-break>だからジリオンをもとに戻して！<end>"
EN: "You can have the Assimilation Hair Ornament. Just turn Zillion back to normal!<end>"
KO: "동화의 머리장식은 줄게. 그러니까 질리온을 원래대로 돌려놔!<end>"

## 2670002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んー、そうは言われてもね。<line-break>どうしようっかな？<line-break>　<line-break>君の願いをかなえてあげたいけど、<line-break>僕にはジリオンちゃんの願いも<line-break>大事なんだよね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Even if you put it that way, I'm not sure. What should I do? I'd like to grant your wish, but Zillion's wish matters to me too.<end>"
KO: "음, 그렇게 말해도 말이지. 어떻게 할까? 네 소원을 들어주고 싶지만 나한텐 질리온의 소원도 중요하거든.<end>"

## 2670003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。なぜ、ここに？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Why are you here?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 왜 여기에?<end>"

## 2670004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハ。たまにはいい場面で<line-break>登場してくれるね、<value:$28><line-break>お陰でいいこと思いついたよ。<line-break><value:$28>のソウルを<line-break>使えばいいんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>この方法ならジリオンとイーシャ<line-break>２人の願いを同時にかなえられる。<end>"
EN: "Ahahaha! You do show up at just the right moment sometimes, <value:$28>. You've given me a wonderful idea. I can use <value:$28>'s Soul. That way, I can grant both Zillion's wish and Iria's at once.<end>"
KO: "아하하하. 가끔은 좋은 타이밍에 등장해 주는군, <value:$28>. 덕분에 좋은 생각이 났어. <value:$28>의 소울을 쓰면 돼. 이 방법이면 질리온과 이샤, 두 사람의 소원을 동시에 이룰 수 있어.<end>"

## 2670005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな…！<line-break>ダメよ。そんなことできないわ。<end>"
EN: "N-no...! That's out of the question.<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가…! 안 돼. 그런 건 못 해.<end>"

## 2670006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞こえたよね、<value:$28>。<line-break>この悲恋を成就させるために、<line-break>ひと肌脱いであげたら？<line-break>もっとも、ソウルを失ったら<line-break>君は消えちゃうんだけどね。アハハハ。<line-break>さあ、どうする<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "You heard her, <value:$28>. Why not lend a hand and bring this tragic romance to a happy end? Of course, if you lose your Soul, you'll disappear. Ahahaha! Well, what will it be, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "들었지, <value:$28>. 이 비극적인 사랑을 이루게 해 주려고 한몫할 생각 없어? 물론 소울을 잃으면 너는 사라지겠지만. 아하하하. 자, 어떻게 할래 <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2670007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分を犠牲に<end>"
EN: "Sacrifice yourself<end>"
KO: "자신을 희생한다<end>"

## 2670008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イーシャを犠牲に<end>"
EN: "Sacrifice Iria<end>"
KO: "이샤를 희생시킨다<end>"

## 2670009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな、<value:$28>…。<line-break>やめて。あたしたちのために<line-break>あなたが犠牲になるなんて…。<line-break>もう大事な人をそんな目に<line-break>あわせたくないの。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしひとりだけ残されるのは<line-break>もうイヤ！<end>"
EN: "No, <value:$28>... Please, don't. You can't sacrifice yourself for us. I don't want anyone I care about to suffer like that ever again. I can't bear to be the only one left behind again!<end>"
KO: "안 돼, <value:$28>…. 그만둬. 우리를 위해 네가 희생된다니…. 더는 소중한 사람을 그런 꼴로 만들고 싶지 않아. 나 혼자만 남는 건 이제 싫어!<end>"

## 2670010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれあれ？<line-break>まだわかってないみたいだね、<line-break>イーシャ。<line-break>君はずっとジリオンにそんな思いを<line-break>させてきたんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>そして今度は<value:$28>に<line-break>同じ思いをさせようとしている。<end>"
EN: "Oh dear. You still don't understand, do you, Iria? That's exactly what you've been putting Zillion through all this time. And now you're about to do the same to <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어라어라? 아직도 이해 못 한 것 같네, 이샤. 넌 계속 질리온에게 그런 마음을 느끼게 했어. 그리고 이번엔 <value:$28>에게 똑같은 마음을 느끼게 하려는 거야.<end>"

## 2670011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気づいていないだろうけど、<line-break>君はとても愛されているんだ。<line-break>君が幼いころからそう望んでいたように。<line-break>だけど愛されることには<line-break>責任がともなうんだよ。<line-break>君にその自覚があるのかな？<end>"
EN: "You may not realize it, but you are dearly loved, just as you've wished to be since you were a child. But being loved comes with responsibility. Are you aware of that?<end>"
KO: "눈치채지 못했겠지만, 너는 아주 많은 사랑을 받고 있어. 어릴 때부터 네가 바라던 대로 말이야. 하지만 사랑받는 데에는 책임이 따른단다. 너에게 그 자각이 있을까?<end>"

## 2670012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフッ。ようやく気づいたみたいだね。<line-break>君の願いに同化の髪飾りが反応している。<line-break>さあ、仕上げといこうか。<end>"
EN: "Heh. It seems you've finally understood. The Assimilation Hair Ornament is responding to your wish. Now, let's finish this.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 드디어 알아챈 모양이네. 네 소원에 동화의 머리장식이 반응하고 있어. 자, 마무리하자.<end>"

## 2670013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな、願っている…。<line-break>それぞれが相手の幸せを想って…。<line-break>決して同時にかなうことのない願いを…。<line-break>それでも、かなわない願いを救うのが<line-break>僕の役目…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone is wishing... each for someone else's happiness... Wishes that can never all be granted at once... Even so, it is my duty to save the wishes that cannot come true...<end>"
KO: "모두 소원을 품고 있어…. 각자 상대의 행복을 바라면서…. 결코 동시에 이루어질 수 없는 소원을…. 그래도 이루어지지 않는 소원을 구하는 게 내 역할이야….<end>"

## 2670014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン！<end>"
EN: "Zillion!<end>"
KO: "질리온!<end>"

## 2670015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イーシャ…。イーシャなのか？<line-break>俺はいったい、どうして…？<end>"
EN: "Iria... Is that you? How did I...?<end>"
KO: "이샤…. 이샤인 거야? 나는 대체, 왜…?<end>"

## 2670016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これですべての願いをかなえたから<line-break>同化の髪飾りはもらっていくよ。<end>"
EN: "Now that I've granted every wish, I'll be taking the Assimilation Hair Ornament.<end>"
KO: "이제 모든 소원을 이뤘으니 동화의 머리장식은 가져갈게.<end>"

## 2670017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、当然だよね。赤の他人のために<line-break>自分のソウルは捨てられない。<line-break>ある意味、最も人間らしい選択だよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, of course. No one would give up a Soul for a complete stranger. In a way, it's the most human choice you could have made.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 당연하지. 생판 남을 위해 자기 소울을 버릴 순 없잖아. 어떤 의미에선 가장 인간다운 선택이야.<end>"

## 2670018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってるわ、<value:$28>…。<line-break>あなたにはもっと大事な役割が<line-break>残っているんだから。<line-break>あなたと一緒に旅ができてよかった。<line-break>さようなら…。<end>"
EN: "I understand, <value:$28>... You still have a more important role to fulfill. I'm glad I got to travel with you. Goodbye...<end>"
KO: "알고 있어, <value:$28>…. 너에겐 더 중요한 역할이 남아 있으니까. 너와 함께 여행할 수 있어서 좋았어. 안녕….<end>"

## 2670019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec267-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束どおり、同化の髪飾りは<line-break>僕がもらっていくよ。<line-break>まさか異論はないよね？　アハハハ。<end>"
EN: "As promised, I'll be taking the Assimilation Hair Ornament. Surely you don't object? Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "약속대로 동화의 머리장식은 내가 가져갈게. 설마 이견은 없겠지? 아하하하.<end>"

## 2680001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec268-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。あなたと同じように<line-break>星を見に来たわけじゃないの。<line-break>　<line-break>お別れを言いに来たのよ。<line-break>もう残された時間がないから…。<line-break>うっ…！<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I didn't come here to see the stars like you did. I came to say goodbye. I don't have any time left... Ngh...!<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 당신처럼 별을 보러 온 건 아니에요. 작별 인사를 하러 왔어요. 이제 남은 시간이 없으니까…. 윽…!<end>"

## 2680002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec268-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメよ。あたしにはわかるの。<line-break>もう同化の髪飾りも、<line-break>あたしの想いを支えられない。<line-break>あたしはこれまでずっとジリオンへの<line-break>想いだけで生きてきた。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あなたと一緒に旅をするように<line-break>なってから、あたしの中で少しずつ<line-break>あなたへの想いが大きくなって…。<end>"
EN: "No, it's no use. I can tell. Even the Hair Ornament of Assimilation can't sustain my feelings anymore. Until now, my feelings for Zillion were all that kept me going. But since I started traveling with you, my feelings for you have grown little by little...<end>"
KO: "안 돼. 난 알아. 이제 동화의 머리장식으로도 내 마음을 붙잡아 둘 수 없어. 난 지금까지 줄곧 질리온을 향한 마음만으로 살아왔어. 하지만 너와 함께 여행하기 시작한 뒤로 내 안에서 조금씩 너를 향한 마음이 커져 가고 있어….<end>"

## 2680003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec268-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、<value:$28>。<line-break>本当はこんなこと言わずに<line-break>黙って消えるつもりだった…。<line-break>でも、もう自分を偽り続けることは<line-break>できないの。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえこの身が消滅しても<line-break>あなたへの想いは止められない。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. I meant to disappear without ever telling you any of this... But I can't keep lying to myself. Even if this body ceases to exist, I can't stop loving you.<end>"
KO: "미안해, <value:$28>. 사실은 이런 말도 하지 않고 조용히 사라질 생각이었어…. 하지만 더는 나 자신을 계속 속일 수 없어. 설령 이 몸이 소멸하더라도 당신을 향한 마음은 멈출 수 없어.<end>"

## 2680004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec268-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしは、もっとあなたと旅をして<line-break>もっと語り合って、そしてもう少しだけ<line-break>あなたと生きたかった。<end>"
EN: "I wanted to travel with you longer, to talk with you more... and to live beside you just a little longer.<end>"
KO: "나는 더 당신과 여행하고 더 이야기를 나누고, 그리고 조금만 더 당신과 함께 살고 싶었어.<end>"

## 2680005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec268-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さようなら、<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Goodbye, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 2690001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec269-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜだ、イオンズ！？<line-break>なぜ、俺のことをイーシャに話した？<line-break>そのせいであいつが…俺の代わりに…。<end>"
EN: "Why, Ions?! Why did you tell Iria about me? Because of you, she... in my place...<end>"
KO: "왜야, 이온즈!? 왜 내 얘기를 이샤한테 했어? 그 때문에 그 녀석이… 나 대신….<end>"

## 2690002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec269-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆ、許してくれ、ジリオン。<line-break>わしにもわからんのだ…。どうして、<line-break>こんなことになってしまったのか。<end>"
EN: "F-forgive me, Zillion. I don't understand it myself... I don't know how it came to this.<end>"
KO: "요, 용서해다오, 질리온. 나도 모르겠다…. 어째서 이런 일이 되어버린 건지.<end>"

## 2690003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec269-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待っててくれ、イーシャ。<line-break>必ずもとに戻してやる。<line-break>この俺が必ず…。<end>"
EN: "Wait for me, Iria. I'll put you back the way you were. I swear I will...<end>"
KO: "기다려 줘, 이샤. 반드시 원래대로 되돌려 놓을게. 내가 반드시….<end>"

## 2690004

## 2700001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec270-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはそうと、お主。<line-break>イーシャという娘を知らんかね？<line-break>　<line-break>そうだな。歳は２０歳前後。<line-break>髪は茶色で、可愛い顔をしているが、<line-break>短剣やナイフの扱いが得意でな。<end>"
EN: "By the way, do you know a young woman named Iria? Let's see... She'd be about twenty, with brown hair and a pretty face, but she's skilled with daggers and knives.<end>"
KO: "그건 그렇고 자네. 이샤라는 아가씨를 모르나? 그렇군. 나이는 스무 살 전후. 갈색 머리에 귀여운 얼굴이지만 단검이나 나이프를 잘 다루지.<end>"

## 2700002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec270-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。知らぬか。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you don't know her.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 모르는가.<end>"

## 2700003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec270-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、なに。<line-break>そのイーシャが最近、天経院の周りで<line-break>ダークエルフと一緒にいたと聞いてな。<line-break>ダークエルフには<line-break>善悪の区別なく、殺して回る<line-break>物騒な奴もいる。<line-break>イーシャがそんな奴と<line-break>関わりになっているとは思えんが<line-break>ちと心配になってな。<end>"
EN: "No, it's just that I heard Iria has been around the Tenkeiin with a Dark Elf lately. Some Dark Elves are dangerous sorts who go around killing without regard for good or evil. I can't imagine her getting mixed up with anyone like that, but it has me a little worried.<end>"
KO: "아니, 별건 아니네. 그 이샤가 최근 천경원 주변에서 다크엘프와 함께 있었다고 들어서 말일세. 다크엘프 중에는 선악을 가리지 않고 마구 죽이고 다니는 위험한 자도 있지. 이샤가 그런 자와 엮였으리라 생각하진 않지만 조금 걱정이 되어서 말이야.<end>"

## 2700004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec270-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしにも経験があるが<line-break>若いころは危険をかえりみずに<line-break>無茶をすることもあるからな。<line-break>何事もなければよいが…。<end>"
EN: "I know from experience that the young can be reckless, heedless of danger. I only hope nothing has happened to her...<end>"
KO: "나도 경험이 있지만 젊을 때는 위험도 아랑곳하지 않고 무리하기도 하지. 아무 일 없으면 좋겠군….<end>"

## 2710001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（イオンズから聞いた<line-break>　イーシャという娘のことが<line-break>　気になるな…。）<end>"
EN: "(I can't help wondering about that girl Iria, the one Ions told me about...)<end>"
KO: "(이온즈에게 들은 이샤라는 여자아이가 신경 쓰이네….)<end>"

## 2710002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にジリオンを<line-break>よみがえらせてくれるのね？<end>"
EN: "You really will bring Zillion back, won't you?<end>"
KO: "정말 질리온을 되살려 주는 거지?<end>"

## 2710003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無論だ。その返礼として、<line-break>君のなかにある闇の神器、<line-break>同化の髪飾りはいただくがね。<line-break>あと、彼が解放されると同時に<line-break>今度はソウルを失った君が<line-break>あのクリスタルに入ることになる。<line-break>その覚悟はできているかい？<end>"
EN: "Of course. In return, I'll take the Dark Artifact within you: the Hair Ornament of Assimilation. And the moment he's freed, you'll lose your Soul and enter that crystal in his place. Are you prepared for that?<end>"
KO: "물론이지. 그 대가로 네 안의 어둠의 신기, 동화의 머리장식은 받겠다. 그리고 그가 해방되는 순간 이번엔 소울을 잃은 네가 저 크리스털 안에 들어가게 돼. 각오는 됐나?<end>"

## 2710004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構わないわ。やってちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "That's fine. Do it.<end>"
KO: "상관없어. 해 줘.<end>"

## 2710005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな酔狂につきあうのも久しぶりだな。<line-break>ま、俺は同化の髪飾りが手に入れば<line-break>文句はないがね。始めるぞ。<end>"
EN: "It's been a while since I've gone along with madness like this. Still, as long as I get the Hair Ornament of Assimilation, I've no complaints. Let's begin.<end>"
KO: "이런 별난 일에 어울리는 것도 오랜만이군. 뭐, 난 동화의 머리장식만 손에 넣으면 불만은 없어. 시작하자.<end>"

## 2710006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お初にお目にかかる。たしかイーシャは<line-break>ジリオン…と呼んでいたかな。<line-break>俺はジュサプブロス。<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you. Iria called you Zillion, if I recall. I'm Jusabros.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠소. 분명 이샤는 질리온…이라고 불렀던가. 나는 주사프브로스다.<end>"

## 2710007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはどうでもいい。<line-break>なぜ、イーシャの願いをかなえた？<end>"
EN: "That doesn't matter. Why did you grant Iria's wish?<end>"
KO: "그런 건 아무래도 좋아. 왜 이샤의 소원을 이루어줬지?<end>"

## 2710008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい。<line-break>俺はクリスタルから<line-break>君を解放してやったんだぜ？<line-break>礼を言うのならともかく、<line-break>非難するのは筋違いだと思うがね。<end>"
EN: "Now, now. I freed you from the crystal, didn't I? You ought to be thanking me. Blaming me seems rather unreasonable.<end>"
KO: "이봐. 나는 크리스탈에서 너를 해방시켜 줬다고? 감사를 한다면 몰라도 비난하는 건 번지수를 잘못 짚은 것 같은데.<end>"

## 2710009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前がイーシャをそそのかしたのか？<end>"
EN: "Did you put Iria up to this?<end>"
KO: "네가 이샤를 부추긴 거냐?<end>"

## 2710010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が勝手に選んだ自己犠牲の道が、<line-break>イーシャには理解できなかった。<line-break>それだけのことだよ。<line-break>君にとっての正解が、<line-break>他者にとっても正解とは限らない。<line-break>そうだろ？<line-break>まあ、そもそもこの世に正解なんて<line-break>存在しないんだがね。<line-break>　<line-break>結局、あるのは行動とその結果だけ。<line-break>おわかりかな、ジリオン君？<end>"
EN: "Iria couldn't understand the path of self-sacrifice you chose for yourself. That's all. What seems right to you isn't necessarily right to anyone else. Wouldn't you agree? Of course, there are no right answers in this world to begin with. In the end, all we have are actions and their consequences. Do you follow, Zillion?<end>"
KO: "네가 멋대로 선택한 자기희생의 길을 이샤는 이해하지 못했어. 그뿐이야. 네게 정답인 것이 다른 사람에게도 정답이라는 법은 없지. 그렇잖아? 애초에 이 세상에 정답 따위 존재하지 않지만 말이야. 결국 있는 것은 행동과 그 결과뿐이다. 알겠나, 질리온 군?<end>"

## 2710011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "減らず口はそこまでだ。<line-break>同化の髪飾りは返してもらう。<end>"
EN: "That's enough of your glib talk. Give back the Hair Ornament of Assimilation.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 소리는 거기까지다. 동화의 머리장식은 돌려받겠다.<end>"

## 2710012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここで君を始末しても<line-break>契約違反にはならないよな。<line-break>きちんと復活させてやったんだから。<end>"
EN: "If I dispose of you here, that won't violate the contract, will it? I did resurrect you exactly as promised.<end>"
KO: "여기서 너를 처리해도 계약 위반은 아니겠지. 제대로 부활시켜 줬으니까.<end>"

## 2710013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや。誰かと思えば…。<line-break>こんな所で無限のソウルの持ち主が<line-break>しゃしゃり出てきたか。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. Look who it is... The bearer of the Infinite Soul, barging in here of all places.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런. 누군가 했더니…. 이런 곳에서 무한의 소울을 지닌 자가 나서다니.<end>"

## 2710014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ働きは好きじゃないが、<line-break>降りかかる火の粉ぐらいは<line-break>払っておかないとね。<end>"
EN: "I don't care to work for free, but I should at least deal with threats that come my way.<end>"
KO: "공짜로 일하는 건 싫지만, 날아드는 불똥 정도는 털어 내야지.<end>"

## 2710015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まいったな。<line-break>イーシャの願いをかなえるのに<line-break>魔力を使いすぎたか。<line-break>この状態で無限のソウルの持ち主を<line-break>相手にするのは分が悪すぎる。<line-break>ここは逃げの一手だね。<end>"
EN: "This is awkward. Did I use too much magic granting Iria's wish? In this state, the odds are far too poor against the bearer of the Infinite Soul. Time to run.<end>"
KO: "난감하네. 이샤의 소원을 이루느라 마력을 너무 썼나. 이 상태로 무한의 소울을 지닌 자를 상대하기엔 너무 불리해. 여기선 도망치는 수밖에 없겠군.<end>"

## 2710016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て、逃がすか！<end>"
EN: "Wait! You're not getting away!<end>"
KO: "기다려, 놓칠까 보냐!<end>"

## 2710017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同化の髪飾りは手に入れたし、<line-break>俺はここで失礼させていただくよ。<end>"
EN: "I have the Hair Ornament of Assimilation, so I'll take my leave.<end>"
KO: "동화의 머리장식도 손에 넣었으니 난 여기서 실례하지.<end>"

## 2710018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、逃げられたか。<end>"
EN: "Damn, he got away.<end>"
KO: "큭, 놓쳤나.<end>"

## 2710019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助かった。礼を言う。<line-break>よければ名前を聞かせてくれ。<end>"
EN: "You saved me. Thank you. If you don't mind, may I have your name?<end>"
KO: "살았다. 고맙다. 괜찮다면 이름을 알려 줘.<end>"

## 2710020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>俺はジリオン。<line-break>縁があったら、また会おう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I'm Zillion. Perhaps we'll meet again someday.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 나는 질리온. 인연이 있으면 다시 만나자.<end>"

## 2710021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。<line-break>あのジュサプブロスという男を<line-break>追うつもりだ。<line-break>奴が持って逃げた同化の髪飾りを<line-break>絶対に取り返す。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I intend to pursue that man, Jusabros. I will get back the Hair Ornament of Assimilation he fled with, no matter what.<end>"
KO: "그래. 주사프브로스라는 남자를 쫓을 거야. 녀석이 갖고 달아난 동화의 머리장식을 반드시 되찾겠어.<end>"

## 2710022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の仲間に？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>いいのか？　奴は手強いぞ。<line-break>俺が一緒にいると、<line-break>お前に迷惑がかかるかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "You want me to join <value:$28>? Are you sure? He's a formidable opponent. Having me with you may only cause you trouble.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 동료로? 괜찮겠어? 녀석은 강해. 내가 같이 있으면 네게 폐가 될지도 몰라.<end>"

## 2710023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも構わないと言うのか…？<end>"
EN: "And you still don't mind...?<end>"
KO: "그래도 상관없다는 건가…?<end>"

## 2710024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。どうやら<value:$28>が<line-break>正しいようだな。<line-break><line-break>長い間眠りつづけていた俺の身体では<line-break>奴を追うことすら、<line-break>ままならないようだ。<end>"
EN: "Damn. It seems <value:$28> was right. After sleeping for so long, this body of mine can't even manage to pursue him.<end>"
KO: "큭. 아무래도 <value:$28> 말이 맞는 것 같군. 오랫동안 잠들어 있던 내 몸으로는 놈을 쫓는 것조차 쉽지 않은 모양이다.<end>"

## 2710025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。恩に着る。<line-break>よろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm in your debt. I'll be counting on you.<end>"
KO: "고맙다. 신세 지겠군. 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 2710026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷の転送機？<line-break>その機械なら俺がどこにいても<line-break>呼び寄せられるのか…。<line-break>わかった。俺にできることがあれば<line-break>いつでも力を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "The Cat Manor teleporter? That device can summon me no matter where I am...? Very well. Whenever you need me, I'll lend whatever aid I can.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택의 전송기? 그 기계라면 내가 어디 있든 불러올 수 있는 건가…. 알았다. 내가 할 수 있는 일이 있다면 언제든 힘을 보태지.<end>"

## 2710027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec271-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベント13用開終端<end>"

## 2720002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。天経院でのことか？<line-break>そうだな。仲間に加えてもらった以上<line-break>誤解されるのもイヤだしな。<line-break>俺はべつに悪いことをして<line-break>あのクリスタルに<line-break>封じこめられていたわけじゃない。<line-break>ちょっとした事情があったんだ。<line-break>イーシャと俺に関わることでな。<end>"
EN: "Oh. You mean what happened at Tenkeiin? Fair enough. Now that you've taken me into your group, I don't want you getting the wrong idea. I wasn't sealed inside that crystal because I'd done something wrong. There were... circumstances. Something involving Iria and me.<end>"
KO: "아아, 천경원에서 있었던 일 말이지? 그래. 동료로 받아 준 이상 오해받기도 싫으니까. 내가 무슨 나쁜 짓을 해서 그 크리스털에 봉인돼 있던 건 아니야. 조금 사정이 있었지. 이샤와 나에 관한 일이야.<end>"

## 2720003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不思議に思うかもしれんが、<line-break>俺とイーシャは同時に存在できない。<line-break>　<line-break>イーシャがある事件に巻き込まれ、<line-break>自分のソウルを失ってしまったからだ。<end>"
EN: "It may sound strange, but Iria and I can't exist at the same time. She was caught up in an incident and lost her Soul.<end>"
KO: "이상하게 생각할지 모르지만 나와 이샤는 동시에 존재할 수 없다. 이샤가 어떤 사건에 휘말려 자기 소울을 잃어 버렸기 때문이다.<end>"

## 2720004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。本来なら<line-break>ソウルを消された時点で<line-break>その人間はもうダメだ。<line-break>だが、なんだかんだあってな…。<line-break>俺は己がソウルを捨て、神器の力で、<line-break>イーシャを復活させたんだ。<line-break>そして俺はあのクリスタルの中に行き<line-break>イーシャは幸せに暮らす…。<line-break>それで何もかもうまくいくはずだった。<end>"
EN: "Normally, once someone's Soul is erased, that's the end of it. But one thing led to another... I cast aside my own Soul and used the power of a divine artifact to bring Iria back. I would go into that crystal, and Iria would live a happy life. That was supposed to make everything right.<end>"
KO: "그래. 원래라면 소울을 잃은 순간 그 사람은 끝이야. 하지만 여러 일이 있었지…. 나는 내 소울을 버리고 신기의 힘으로 이샤를 부활시켰어. 그리고 나는 그 크리스탈 안으로 들어가고 이샤는 행복하게 살아간다…. 그걸로 모든 게 잘될 줄 알았지.<end>"

## 2720005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、それを知ったイーシャは<line-break>俺が身代わりになっていることに<line-break>耐えきれず、<line-break>ジュサプブロスと取引をして、<line-break>自分がクリスタルに入ってしまった。<end>"
EN: "But when Iria found out, she couldn't bear knowing I'd taken her place. She struck a bargain with Jusabros and entered the crystal herself.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그걸 안 이샤는 내가 대신 희생하고 있다는 걸 견디지 못하고 주사프브로스와 거래해서 자신이 크리스털 안으로 들어가 버렸어.<end>"

## 2720006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺が出れば、あいつが消え、<line-break>あいつが出れば、俺が消える。<line-break>終わりのない堂々めぐりだな…。<end>"
EN: "When I come out, she disappears. When she comes out, I disappear. We just keep going around in circles...<end>"
KO: "내가 나오면 그 녀석이 사라지고, 그 녀석이 나오면 내가 사라진다. 끝없는 제자리걸음이군….<end>"

## 2720007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく今、俺にできるのは<line-break>ジュサプブロスを捜しだし、<line-break>同化の髪飾りを取り返すことだけだ。<line-break>さしあたって<line-break>それ以外にイーシャを<line-break>もとに戻す方法もないんでな。<end>"
EN: "For now, all I can do is find Jusabros and recover the Hair Ornament of Assimilation. At present, there's no other way to restore Iria.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 지금 내가 할 수 있는 건 주사프브로스를 찾아내 동화의 머리장식을 되찾는 것뿐이야. 당장은 그 외에 이샤를 원래대로 되돌릴 방법도 없으니까.<end>"

## 2720008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまん。今日はもう疲れた。<line-break>先に休ませてもらう。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. I'm tired tonight. I'm going to turn in.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 오늘은 이제 지쳤어. 먼저 쉬게 해 줘.<end>"

## 2720009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。まだ起きていた。<line-break>イーシャのことが気になってな。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I was still awake. I was thinking about Iria.<end>"
KO: "그래. 아직 깨어 있었어. 이샤가 신경 쓰여서 말이지.<end>"

## 2720011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリスタルに入っている間、<line-break>俺はずっとイーシャのことを<line-break>想っていた。<line-break>あいつは今、誰を想い、<line-break>何を考えているんだろう？<end>"
EN: "The whole time I was inside the crystal, I thought about Iria. I wonder who she's thinking of now, and what's on her mind.<end>"
KO: "크리스털 안에 있는 동안 나는 줄곧 이샤를 생각했다. 그 녀석은 지금 누구를 생각하고, 무슨 생각을 하고 있을까?<end>"

## 2720012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ。<value:$28>は<line-break>誰か大切な人のことを<line-break>想ったことがあるか？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, <value:$28>. Have you ever found yourself thinking about someone dear to you?<end>"
KO: "있잖아. <value:$28>는 소중한 누군가를 생각해 본 적 있어?<end>"

## 2720013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまん。野暮なことを訊いた。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. I shouldn't have asked.<end>"
KO: "미안. 눈치 없는 걸 물었군.<end>"

## 2720014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は今までイーシャを<line-break>助けようと躍起になっていた。<end>"
EN: "Until now, I've been so desperate to save Iria.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금까지 이샤를 구하려고 필사적이었다.<end>"

## 2720015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、俺が考えているイーシャの幸せと<line-break>あいつが望んでいる本当の幸せは<line-break>もしかすると別なのかもしれない…。<line-break>今、ふと、そう考えていた。<end>"
EN: "But perhaps what I think would make Iria happy isn't the happiness she truly wants... The thought just occurred to me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 내가 생각하는 이샤의 행복과 그 녀석이 바라는 진짜 행복은 어쩌면 다른 걸지도 몰라…. 지금 문득 그렇게 생각했어.<end>"

## 2720016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。<line-break>まずはジュサプブロスから<line-break>同化の髪飾りを取り返さないと…。<line-break>今の俺にできるのは、<line-break>それだけだからな。<end>"
EN: "You're right. First, I have to recover the Hair Ornament of Assimilation from Jusabros. That's all I can do for now.<end>"
KO: "그래. 우선 주사프브로스에게서 동화의 머리장식을 되찾아야 해…. 지금 내가 할 수 있는 건 그것뿐이니까.<end>"

## 2720017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。夜空を眺めていた。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あのクリスタルに入る前は<line-break>星空がこれほど美しいと<line-break>感じることはなかった。<line-break>自分でも不思議なんだ。<line-break>今はなぜだか、素直に<line-break>感動することができる。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. I was looking at the night sky. Before I entered that crystal, I'd never found the stars so beautiful. It's strange, even to me. For some reason, now I can simply let myself be moved by them.<end>"
KO: "그래. 밤하늘을 보고 있었어. 저 크리스털에 들어가기 전에는 별하늘이 이렇게 아름답다고 느낀 적이 없었거든. 나도 신기해. 지금은 왜인지 솔직하게 감동할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2720019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はクリスタルから解放されて<line-break>今日まで、自分が生きているという<line-break>実感がなかった。<line-break>ただ、イーシャのために<line-break>かりそめの命を<line-break>もらっているだけだと…。<end>"
EN: "Ever since I was freed from the crystal, I haven't truly felt alive. I thought this was only a temporary life I'd been given for Iria's sake...<end>"
KO: "나는 크리스털에서 해방된 뒤 오늘까지 내가 살아 있다는 실감이 없었어. 그저 이샤를 위해 임시로 목숨을 받은 것뿐이라고….<end>"

## 2720020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺とイーシャはテジャワの乱で出会い、<line-break>ときに味方として思いをぶつけ合い、<line-break>ときには敵として刃を交えた。<line-break>でも、最後は互いにわかり合えたと<line-break>信じていた。<line-break>　<line-break>だからこそ俺はあいつに<line-break>幸せになってもらいたかった。<end>"
EN: "I met Iria during the Tejava Incident. At times we stood together and laid our feelings bare; at others, we crossed blades as enemies. But in the end, I believed we'd come to understand each other. That was why I wanted her to find happiness.<end>"
KO: "나와 이샤는 테자와의 난에서 만나 때로는 아군으로서 서로의 생각을 부딪치고, 때로는 적으로서 칼을 맞댔다. 그래도 마지막에는 서로 이해했다고 믿었어. 그렇기에 나는 그 녀석이 행복해지길 바랐다.<end>"

## 2720021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は自分が犠牲になることで<line-break>イーシャを幸せにできると<line-break>考えていた。<line-break>それが…結果はどうだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>イーシャは生きることを拒絶して<line-break>俺を復活させ、自分が<line-break>クリスタルに入ってしまった。<end>"
EN: "I thought I could make Iria happy by sacrificing myself. And look how that turned out... Iria refused to go on living. She brought me back and entered the crystal herself.<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 희생되면 이샤를 행복하게 할 수 있다고 생각했어. 그런데 결과는 어땠지. 이샤는 살아가기를 거부하고 나를 부활시킨 뒤, 자신이 크리스털 속에 들어가 버렸어.<end>"

## 2720022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "教えてくれ、<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺は間違っていたのか？<line-break>　<line-break>彼女にとって俺の行為は<line-break>重荷でしかなかったのか？<line-break>それとも俺の自己満足だったのか？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, <value:$28>... Was I wrong? Were my actions nothing but a burden to her? Or did I only do it to satisfy myself?<end>"
KO: "말해 줘, <value:$28>…. 내가 틀렸던 건가? 그녀에게 내 행동은 짐일 뿐이었나? 아니면 내 자기만족이었던 건가?<end>"

## 2720023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっている。<line-break>俺は自分の選択に悔いている<line-break>わけじゃない。<line-break>ただ、イーシャが本当はどんな幸せを<line-break>望んでいたのか。<line-break>そんな疑問がたまに頭をよぎるんだ。<end>"
EN: "I know. It's not that I regret my choice. I just sometimes find myself wondering what kind of happiness Iria truly wanted.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 내 선택을 후회하는 건 아니야. 다만 이샤가 진정 어떤 행복을 바랐는지, 그런 의문이 가끔 머리를 스쳐.<end>"

## 2720024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。<value:$28>。<line-break>俺の行動が正しかったかどうかは<line-break>俺自身にもわからない。<line-break>だけど、イーシャへの想いは真実だ。<line-break>それだけは胸を張って言える。<end>"
EN: "You're right, <value:$28>. Even I don't know whether what I did was right. But my feelings for Iria are real. That much I can say with pride.<end>"
KO: "그래, <value:$28>. 내 행동이 옳았는지는 나도 몰라. 하지만 이샤를 향한 마음은 진심이다. 그것만은 당당히 말할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2720025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>。<line-break>おかげで少し楽になった。<line-break>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. I feel a little better now."
KO: "고마워. <value:$28>. 덕분에 조금 편해졌어."

## 2720026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺とイーシャはテジャワの乱で出会い、<line-break>ときに味方として思いをぶつけ合い、<line-break>ときには敵として刃を交えた。<line-break>でも、最後は互いにわかり合えたと<line-break>信じていた。<line-break>　<line-break>だからこそ俺はあいつに<line-break>幸せになってもらいたかった。<end>"
EN: "I met Iria during the Tejava Incident. At times we stood together and laid our feelings bare; at others, we crossed blades as enemies. But in the end, I believed we'd come to understand each other. That was why I wanted her to find happiness.<end>"
KO: "나와 이샤는 테자와의 난에서 만나 때로는 아군으로서 서로의 생각을 부딪치고, 때로는 적으로서 칼을 맞댔다. 그래도 마지막에는 서로 이해했다고 믿었어. 그렇기에 나는 그 녀석이 행복해지길 바랐다.<end>"

## 2720030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は自分が犠牲になることで<line-break>イーシャを幸せにできると<line-break>思っていた。<line-break>それが…結果はどうだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>イーシャは生きることを拒絶して<line-break>俺を復活させ、自分が<line-break>クリスタルに入ってしまった。<end>"
EN: "I thought I could make Iria happy by sacrificing myself. And look how that turned out... Iria refused to go on living. She brought me back and entered the crystal herself.<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 희생되면 이샤를 행복하게 할 수 있다고 생각했어. 그런데 결과는 어땠지. 이샤는 살아가기를 거부하고 나를 부활시킨 뒤, 자신이 크리스털 속에 들어가 버렸어.<end>"

## 2720031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜだ、<value:$28>…。俺は間違っていたのか？　<line-break>俺が犠牲になればイーシャは救える。それだけが正しい選択だと信じていた。　<line-break>だけど、お前と旅していくうちに<line-break>それだけが答えじゃないと<line-break>俺は思いはじめてきた。<line-break>だとしたらこの先、俺の進むべき道は<line-break>どこにあるんだろう…？<line-break>俺はどうすればいいんだ？<line-break>教えてくれ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Why, <value:$28>...? Was I wrong? I believed there was only one right choice: if I sacrificed myself, Iria would be saved. But as I've traveled with you, I've begun to think that may not be the only answer. If so, where does my path lead from here...? What am I supposed to do? Tell me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "왜지, <value:$28>…. 내가 틀렸던 건가? 내가 희생하면 이샤를 구할 수 있다. 그게 유일하게 옳은 선택이라고 믿었어. 하지만 너와 여행하면서 그게 전부가 아닐지도 모른다고 생각하게 됐어. 그렇다면 앞으로 내가 가야 할 길은 어디에 있는 거지…? 난 어떻게 해야 하지? 알려 줘, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2720032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。お前の言うとおり、<line-break>道はひとつだけじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>俺がまた犠牲となって<line-break>クリスタルに入っても<line-break>やはりイーシャは苦しむだろう。<line-break>そして、また同じ決断を<line-break>繰り返してしまうかもしれない。<line-break>　<line-break>そんな虚しい堂々めぐりなんて<line-break>終わらせるべきなんだ。<end>"
EN: "You're right. There isn't only one path. Even if I sacrificed myself again and returned to the crystal, Iria would still suffer. She might make the same choice all over again. This futile circle has to end.<end>"
KO: "그래. 네 말대로 길은 하나뿐이 아니야. 내가 다시 희생해서 크리스탈에 들어가도 이샤는 역시 괴로워하겠지. 그리고 또 같은 결정을 되풀이할지도 몰라. 그런 허무한 악순환은 끝내야 해.<end>"

## 2720033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺自身が出さなければ<line-break>いけない答えなのに、<line-break>お前に甘えてしまった。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. This was an answer I needed to find for myself, but I leaned on you instead.<end>"
KO: "고맙다, <value:$28>. 내가 스스로 내야 할 답인데 네게 기대고 말았군.<end>"

## 2720034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしかすると、俺はお前を…。<line-break>…いや、いい。<line-break>忘れてくれ。おやすみ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I've come to... No. Never mind. Forget it. Good night.<end>"
KO: "어쩌면 나는 너를….…아니, 됐어. 잊어 줘. 잘 자.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。そんな日が来るといいな。<line-break>俺とイーシャと<value:$28>、<line-break>３人で同じ月を見上げる日が…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. I hope that day comes. The day Iria, <value:$28>, and I can all look up at the same moon together...<end>"
KO: "그래. 그런 날이 오면 좋겠군. 나와 이샤와 <value:$28>, 셋이 같은 달을 올려다보는 날이….<end>"

## 2720039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺が冒険者になった理由？<line-break>さあな。<line-break>もう覚えちゃいないな。<line-break>ただ、冒険者になると決めた時<line-break>親父が遺してくれた剣に誓ったんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>一流の鍛冶屋だった親父に<line-break>負けない人生を歩んでみせるってな。<end>"
EN: "Why did I become an adventurer? Who knows? I don't even remember anymore. But when I made that choice, I swore an oath on the sword my father left me. He was a first-rate blacksmith, and I swore I'd live a life worthy of his.<end>"
KO: "내가 모험자가 된 이유? 글쎄, 이젠 기억도 안 나. 다만 모험자가 되기로 했을 때 아버지가 남겨 준 검에 맹세했지. 일류 대장장이였던 아버지에게 지지 않는 삶을 살겠다고.<end>"

## 2720040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イーシャは<line-break>ダークエルフと取引してまで<line-break>俺を復活させた。<line-break>イーシャをそこまで追い詰めたのは<line-break>たぶん俺自身なんだろうな。<line-break>　<line-break>俺はあいつの苦しみを<line-break>わかってやれなかった…。<line-break>もう同じ過ちを犯したくはない。<end>"
EN: "Iria went so far as to bargain with a Dark Elf to bring me back. I suppose I'm the one who drove her to such lengths. I didn't understand how much she was suffering... I don't want to make the same mistake again.<end>"
KO: "이샤는 다크엘프와 거래까지 해서 나를 부활시켰어. 이샤를 그만큼 몰아붙인 건 아마 나 자신이었겠지. 난 그 애의 고통을 이해해 주지 못했어…. 다시는 같은 실수를 하고 싶지 않아.<end>"

## 2720042

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"

## 2720043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな夜は昔のことを思い出すんだ。<line-break>昔、ロストールで起こった<line-break>テジャワの変のこととかな。<line-break>ファーロス家の失権を狙った内乱…。<line-break>まさかそれに自分が巻き込まれるとは<line-break>想像もしていなかった。<line-break>思えば、あれが<line-break>俺とイーシャの運命を<line-break>変えてしまったんだな…。<end>"
EN: "Nights like this bring back old memories. The Tejava Uprising in Rostorl, for instance. A civil war intended to strip House Pharos of its power... I never imagined I'd be caught up in it. Looking back, that was what changed Iria's fate and mine...<end>"
KO: "이런 밤이면 옛일이 떠오른다. 예전에 로스톨에서 일어난 테자와의 변 같은 것 말이야. 파로스 가문을 실각시키려 한 내란…. 내가 거기에 휘말릴 줄은 상상도 못했지. 돌이켜 보면 그 일이 나와 이샤의 운명을 바꿔 버렸구나….<end>"

## 2720044

EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあな。<line-break>イーシャを助け出したあとのことは<line-break>まだ決めてない…。<line-break>しばらく<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>旅をするのもいいかもな。<end>"
EN: "I don't know. I haven't decided what I'll do after I rescue Iria... Maybe I'll travel with you for a while, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "글쎄. 이샤를 구한 뒤의 일은 아직 정하지 않았어…. 한동안 <value:$28>과 같이 여행하는 것도 괜찮겠지.<end>"

## 2720047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同化の髪飾りは<line-break>闇の円卓の騎士・ラハブの魔道器だ。<line-break>ラハブの異名は、ひとつにするもの…。<line-break>テジャワの変の陰でうごめいていたのも<line-break>同化の髪飾りだった。<line-break>　<line-break>そのせいで多くの人が死に、<line-break>ひとりの幼子は、<line-break>母親と光と未来を失ってしまった…。<end>"
EN: "The Hair Ornament of Assimilation is a magical instrument belonging to Rahab, a Knight of the Dark Round Table. Rahab is known as the One Who Makes All One... The ornament was also at work behind the Tejava Uprising. Because of it, many people died, and one small child was left without a mother, without light, and without a future...<end>"
KO: "동화의 머리장식은 어둠의 원탁 기사 라하브의 마도기다. 라하브의 이명은 하나로 만드는 자…. 테자와의 변 뒤에서 꿈틀거리던 것도 동화의 머리장식이었다. 그 때문에 많은 사람이 죽었고, 한 어린아이는 어머니와 빛과 미래를 잃고 말았다….<end>"

## 2720049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス様のことを思い出していた。<line-break>あの人に会ったのも<line-break>テジャワの変でだった。<line-break>凛とした、しかしやさしい人だった。<line-break>イーシャは姉のように慕ってた。<line-break>俺もあの人に命を助けられた。<line-break>正直、少し憧れていたのかもな。<line-break>あの人のためもあって<line-break>俺たちはテジャワの変を戦ったんだ…。<end>"
EN: "I was thinking about Lady Eris. I met her during the Tejava Uprising. She had such dignity, but she was kind as well. Iria loved her like an older sister. She saved my life, too. To be honest, perhaps I admired her a little. She was one of the reasons we fought in the uprising...<end>"
KO: "에리스 님을 떠올리고 있었다. 그분을 만난 것도 테자와의 변 때였지. 당당하면서도 다정한 분이었어. 이샤는 친언니처럼 따랐고, 나도 그분에게 목숨을 구원받았지. 솔직히 조금 동경하고 있었는지도 모르겠군. 그분을 위해서이기도 해서 우리는 테자와의 변에서 싸웠어….<end>"

JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。夜空を眺めていた。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あのクリスタルに入る前は<line-break>星空がこれほど美しいと<line-break>感じることはなかった。<line-break>自分でも不思議なんだ。<line-break>今はなぜだか、素直に<line-break>感動することができる。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. I was looking at the night sky. Before I entered that crystal, I'd never found the stars so beautiful. It's strange, even to me. For some reason, now I can simply let myself be moved by them.<end>"
KO: "그래. 밤하늘을 보고 있었어. 저 크리스털에 들어가기 전에는 별하늘이 이렇게 아름답다고 느낀 적이 없었거든. 나도 신기해. 지금은 왜인지 솔직하게 감동할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2720052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イーシャとはテジャワの変で出会った。<line-break>ときに味方として思いをぶつけ合い、<line-break>ときには敵として刃を交えた。<line-break>でも、最後は互いにわかり合えたと<line-break>信じていた。<line-break>　<line-break>だからこそ俺はあいつに<line-break>幸せになってもらいたかった。<end>"
EN: "I met Iria during the Tejava Uprising. At times we stood together and laid our feelings bare; at others, we crossed blades as enemies. But in the end, I believed we'd come to understand each other. That was why I wanted her to find happiness.<end>"
KO: "이샤와는 테자와의 변에서 만났어. 때로는 동료로 서로의 마음을 부딪쳤고, 때로는 적으로 칼을 맞댔지. 그래도 마지막에는 서로 이해했다고 믿었어. 그래서 더욱 그 애가 행복해지길 바랐던 거야.<end>"

## 2720053

## 2720054

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。夜空を眺めていた。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あのクリスタルに入る前は<line-break>星空がこれほど美しいと<line-break>感じることはなかった。<line-break>自分でも不思議なんだ。<line-break>今はなぜだか、素直に<line-break>感動することができる。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. I was looking at the night sky. Before I entered that crystal, I'd never found the stars so beautiful. It's strange, even to me. For some reason, now I can simply let myself be moved by them.<end>"
KO: "그래. 밤하늘을 보고 있었어. 저 크리스털에 들어가기 전에는 별하늘이 이렇게 아름답다고 느낀 적이 없었거든. 나도 신기해. 지금은 왜인지 솔직하게 감동할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2720060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はクリスタルから解放されて<line-break>今日まで、自分が生きているという<line-break>実感がなかった。<line-break>ただ、イーシャのために<line-break>かりそめの命を<line-break>もらっているだけだと…。<end>"
EN: "Ever since I was freed from the crystal, I haven't truly felt alive. I thought this was only a temporary life I'd been given for Iria's sake...<end>"
KO: "나는 크리스털에서 해방된 뒤 오늘까지 내가 살아 있다는 실감이 없었어. 그저 이샤를 위해 임시로 목숨을 받은 것뿐이라고….<end>"

## 2720061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イーシャとはテジャワの変で出会った。<line-break>ときに味方として思いをぶつけ合い、<line-break>ときには敵として刃を交えた。<line-break>でも、最後は互いにわかり合えたと<line-break>信じていた。<line-break>　<line-break>だからこそ俺はあいつに<line-break>幸せになってもらいたかった。<end>"
EN: "I met Iria during the Tejava Uprising. At times we stood together and laid our feelings bare; at others, we crossed blades as enemies. But in the end, I believed we'd come to understand each other. That was why I wanted her to find happiness.<end>"
KO: "이샤와는 테자와의 변에서 만났어. 때로는 동료로 서로의 마음을 부딪쳤고, 때로는 적으로 칼을 맞댔지. 그래도 마지막에는 서로 이해했다고 믿었어. 그래서 더욱 그 애가 행복해지길 바랐던 거야.<end>"

## 2720062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は自分が犠牲になることで<line-break>イーシャを幸せにできると<line-break>考えていた。<line-break>それが…結果はどうだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>イーシャは生きることを拒絶して<line-break>俺を復活させ、自分が<line-break>クリスタルに入ってしまった。<end>"
EN: "I thought I could make Iria happy by sacrificing myself. And look how that turned out... Iria refused to go on living. She brought me back and entered the crystal herself.<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 희생되면 이샤를 행복하게 할 수 있다고 생각했어. 그런데 결과는 어땠지. 이샤는 살아가기를 거부하고 나를 부활시킨 뒤, 자신이 크리스털 속에 들어가 버렸어.<end>"

## 2720063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "教えてくれ、<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺は間違っていたのか？<line-break>　<line-break>彼女にとって俺の行為は<line-break>重荷でしかなかったのか？<line-break>それとも俺の自己満足だったのか？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, <value:$28>... Was I wrong? Were my actions nothing but a burden to her? Or did I only do it to satisfy myself?<end>"
KO: "말해 줘, <value:$28>…. 내가 틀렸던 건가? 그녀에게 내 행동은 짐일 뿐이었나? 아니면 내 자기만족이었던 건가?<end>"

## 2720064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっている。<line-break>俺は自分の選択を悔いている<line-break>わけじゃない。<line-break>ただ、イーシャが本当は<line-break>どんな幸せを望んでいたのか。<line-break>そんな疑問がたまに頭をよぎるんだ。<end>"
EN: "I know. It's not that I regret my choice. I just sometimes find myself wondering what kind of happiness Iria truly wanted.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 내 선택을 후회하는 건 아니야. 다만 이샤가 진정 어떤 행복을 바랐는지, 그런 의문이 가끔 머리를 스쳐.<end>"

## 2720065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。<value:$28>。<line-break>俺の行動が正しかったかどうかは<line-break>俺自身にもわからない。<line-break>だけど、イーシャへの想いは真実だ。<line-break>それだけは胸を張って言える。<end>"
EN: "You're right, <value:$28>. Even I don't know whether what I did was right. But my feelings for Iria are real. That much I can say with pride.<end>"
KO: "그래, <value:$28>. 내 행동이 옳았는지는 나도 몰라. 하지만 이샤를 향한 마음은 진심이다. 그것만은 당당히 말할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2720066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>。<line-break>おかげで少し楽になった。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. I feel a little better now.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 덕분에 조금 편해졌어.<end>"

## 2720067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時々、ふと思うことがある。<line-break>人は大なり小なり、いつも<line-break>決断を迫られている。<line-break>自分の下した判断に満足することも、<line-break>逆に悔やむこともあるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>その繰り返しの中で人は生きていく。<line-break>…かなうのならば、正しい選択を<line-break>俺は見つけたい。<line-break>イーシャと俺…。<line-break>どちらも悔やむことがない選択を。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes a thought comes to me. In matters great and small, people are always being forced to choose. Sometimes we're content with our decisions; other times, we regret them. We live our lives through that endless succession of choices. If such a thing is possible, I want to find the right one―a choice that neither Iria nor I will regret.<end>"
KO: "가끔 문득 생각할 때가 있다. 사람은 크든 작든 늘 결단을 요구받는다. 자신이 내린 판단에 만족하기도 하고, 반대로 후회하기도 하겠지. 그 반복 속에서 사람은 살아간다. …가능하다면 나는 올바른 선택을 찾고 싶다. 이샤와 나…. 어느 쪽도 후회하지 않을 선택을.<end>"

## 2720069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가….<end>"

## 2720070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時々、ふと思うことがある。<line-break>人は大なり小なり、いつも<line-break>決断を迫られている。<line-break>自分の下した判断に満足することも、<line-break>逆に悔やむこともあるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>その繰り返しの中で人は生きていく。<line-break>…かなうのならば、正しい選択を<line-break>俺は見つけたい。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes a thought comes to me. In matters great and small, people are always being forced to choose. Sometimes we're content with our decisions; other times, we regret them. We live our lives through that endless succession of choices. If such a thing is possible, I want to find the right one.<end>"
KO: "가끔 문득 생각할 때가 있다. 사람은 크든 작든 늘 결단을 요구받는다. 자신이 내린 판단에 만족할 때도, 반대로 후회할 때도 있겠지. 그 반복 속에서 사람은 살아간다. …가능하다면 나는 옳은 선택을 찾아내고 싶다.<end>"

## 2720071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、大事な人を<line-break>悲しませることのない選択を…。<end>"
EN: "A choice that will never again bring sorrow to someone dear to me...<end>"
KO: "이제 소중한 사람을 슬프게 하지 않는 선택을….<end>"

## 2720072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親兄弟はいるのかって？<line-break>…いや。親父もおふくろも戦争で<line-break>死んじまったし、兄弟はいない。<line-break>親父は鍛冶屋で腕は一流だった。<line-break>ガキのころ<line-break>大事なことを教わったよ。<line-break>どんなに腕がよくても鍛冶は<line-break>ひとりじゃできない。<line-break>信頼できる仲間を探せって、な。<end>"
EN: "You're asking if I have any family? No. My father and mother both died in the war, and I have no brothers or sisters. My father was a first-rate blacksmith. He taught me something important when I was a boy: no matter how skilled you are, you can't practice the craft alone. Find companions you can trust. That's what he told me.<end>"
KO: "부모나 형제가 있냐고? …아니. 아버지도 어머니도 전쟁에서 돌아가셨고 형제도 없어. 아버지는 대장장이였고 솜씨는 일류였지. 어릴 때 중요한 걸 가르쳐 주셨어. 아무리 솜씨가 좋아도 대장일은 혼자 할 수 없으니 믿을 수 있는 동료를 찾으라고 말이야.<end>"

## 2720073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっとクリスタルの中にいたんで<line-break>月夜が懐かしくてな。<line-break>思わず眺めていた。<end>"
EN: "After being inside that crystal for so long, I missed nights like this. I couldn't help stopping to look at the moon.<end>"
KO: "계속 크리스털 안에 있었더니 달밤이 그리워서 말이야. 나도 모르게 바라보고 있었어.<end>"

## 2720074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec272-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はクリスタルから解放されて<line-break>今日まで、自分が生きているという<line-break>実感がなかった。<line-break>ただ、イーシャのために<line-break>かりそめの命を<line-break>もらっているだけだと…。<end>"
EN: "Ever since I was freed from the crystal, I haven't truly felt alive. I thought this was only a temporary life I'd been given for Iria's sake...<end>"
KO: "나는 크리스털에서 해방된 뒤 오늘까지 내가 살아 있다는 실감이 없었어. 그저 이샤를 위해 임시로 목숨을 받은 것뿐이라고….<end>"

## 2730001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec273-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世話になったな、<value:$28>。<line-break>ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I owe you a great deal, <value:$28>. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "신세 졌다, <value:$28>. 고맙다.<end>"

## 2730002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec273-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこに行くのかって？<line-break>…わかっているだろ。<line-break>天経院だ…。<line-break>俺がイーシャを復活させて<line-break>クリスタルに入れば、<line-break>　<line-break>今度はイーシャが俺を救うために<line-break>クリスタルに入ってしまう。<line-break>　<line-break>同じことの繰り返しだ。<line-break>まるで止まることのない<line-break>振り子のようにな。<end>"
EN: "Where am I going? You already know. To Tenkeiin. If I bring Iria back and enter the crystal, she'll only enter it herself to save me. We'd just keep repeating the same thing, like a pendulum that never stops swinging.<end>"
KO: "어디로 가냐고? …알고 있잖아. 천경원이다…. 내가 이샤를 부활시키고 크리스탈에 들어가면, 이번엔 이샤가 나를 구하려고 크리스탈에 들어가 버려. 같은 일의 반복이야. 마치 멈추지 않는 진자처럼.<end>"

## 2730003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec273-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、決めたんだ。<line-break>この果てしない輪廻に<line-break>俺の手で終止符を打つと…。<line-break>俺とイーシャは永遠にひとつになる。<line-break>あのクリスタルの中で…。<end>"
EN: "So I've made my decision. I'll put an end to this endless cycle with my own hands. Iria and I will become one forever, inside that crystal.<end>"
KO: "그래서 결심했어. 이 끝없는 윤회에 내 손으로 마침표를 찍겠다고… 나와 이샤는 영원히 하나가 된다. 저 크리스털 안에서…<end>"

## 2730004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec273-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっている。俺もできれば最後まで<line-break><value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>冒険を続けたかった…。<line-break>でも、俺はもうイーシャを<line-break>あのままにしておくことはできない。<line-break>さよならだ。旅の幸運を祈っている。<end>"
EN: "I know. If I could, I'd have liked to keep adventuring with you to the very end, <value:$28>. But I can't leave Iria like that any longer. Goodbye. I wish you luck on your journey.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 나도 가능하다면 끝까지 <value:$28>와 함께 모험을 계속하고 싶었어…. 하지만 이제 이샤를 그대로 둘 수는 없어. 작별이다. 여행에 행운이 있길 빌게.<end>"

## 2730005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec273-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんよ。イーシャ…。<line-break>長い間、寂しい想いをさせて。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, Iria, for leaving you lonely all this time.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 이샤…. 오랫동안 외롭게 해서.<end>"

## 2730006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec273-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、お互いに苦しむこともない。<line-break>これからはずっと一緒だ。<end>"
EN: "Neither of us has to suffer anymore. From now on, we'll always be together.<end>"
KO: "이제 서로 괴로워할 일도 없어. 앞으로는 계속 함께야.<end>"

## 2730007

## 2740001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec274-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　よかった…。<line-break>　　　　どうにか声が届いたようだ。<line-break>　　　　　　俺の名はジリオン。<end>"
EN: "Thank goodness... It seems my voice reached you somehow. My name is Zillion.<end>"
KO: "다행이다…. 어떻게든 목소리가 닿은 모양이군. 내 이름은 질리온.<end>"

## 2740002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec274-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　<line-break>　　　そうだ。アルノートゥンの天経院で<line-break>　　　　お前が見たあのクリスタル…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　俺は今もあの中で<line-break>　　　　　　眠りについている。<end>"
EN: "Yes. That crystal you saw at the Tenkeiin in Alnortun... I still lie sleeping within it.<end>"
KO: "그래. 알노툰의 천경원에서 네가 본 그 크리스털…. 나는 지금도 그 안에서 잠들어 있다.<end>"

## 2740003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec274-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　<line-break>　　お前に頼みたいことがあって来たんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　勝手な願いだとわかっているが、<line-break>　　　イーシャを支えてやってほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　今までイーシャは<line-break>　　同化の髪飾りの力と俺への想いだけで<line-break>　　　　　　存在を保ってきた。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　だけど、少しずつイーシャは<line-break>　　　お前のことを想い、お前のために<line-break>　　　　　生き始めようとしている。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　お前への想いで同化の髪飾りの力を<line-break>　　　維持できるのなら、俺はそれを望む。<end>"
EN: "I came because I have a favor to ask of you. I know it is selfish of me, but I want you to be there for Iria. Until now, only the power of the Assimilation Hair Ornament and her feelings for me have sustained her existence. But little by little, she is developing feelings for you and beginning to live for your sake. If her feelings for you can sustain the power of the Assimilation Hair Ornament, then that is what I want.<end>"
KO: "네게 부탁할 일이 있어서 왔어. 제멋대로인 부탁이라는 건 알지만 이샤를 지탱해 줬으면 해. 지금까지 이샤는 동화의 머리장식 힘과 나를 향한 마음만으로 존재를 유지해 왔어. 하지만 조금씩 이샤는 너를 생각하고, 너를 위해 살아가려 하고 있어. 너를 향한 마음으로 동화의 머리장식 힘을 유지할 수 있다면 나는 그것을 바란다.<end>"

## 2740004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec274-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　わかっている。イーシャは優しい女だ。<line-break>　　だからこそ、俺への義務や責任じゃなく、<line-break>　　あいつには自分の道を選んでほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>　　お前とならイーシャも自分を偽らず<line-break>　　　　　素直に生きてゆける。<line-break>　　あいつの幸せ。それだけが俺の願いだ。<end>"
EN: "I know. Iria is a kind woman. That is precisely why I want her to choose her own path, not act out of duty or responsibility to me. With you, Iria can be true to herself and live honestly. Her happiness is all I wish for.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 이샤는 상냥한 여자다. 그렇기에 나에 대한 의무나 책임이 아니라, 그 녀석 스스로 자신의 길을 선택했으면 해. 너와 함께라면 이샤도 자신을 속이지 않고 솔직하게 살아갈 수 있어. 그 녀석의 행복. 내가 바라는 건 그것뿐이다.<end>"

## 2740005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec274-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　ありがとう。<value:$28>。<line-break>　　イーシャのことを頼む。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. Please take care of Iria.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 이샤를 부탁한다.<end>"

## 2750002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec275-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん、星を見ていたんじゃないわ。<line-break>ちょっと寝つけなかったから<line-break>昨日見た夢を思い出していたの。<line-break>そう。ジリオンの夢…。<line-break>せっかく夢で会えたのに<line-break>あいつはただ微笑んでいただけ。<end>"
EN: "No, I wasn't looking at the stars. I couldn't sleep, so I was thinking about the dream I had last night. It was about Zillion... Even though I finally got to see him in a dream, all he did was smile.<end>"
KO: "아니, 별을 보고 있던 게 아니야. 잠이 잘 안 와서 어제 꾼 꿈을 떠올리고 있었어. 그래. 질리온의 꿈…. 모처럼 꿈에서 만났는데 그 녀석은 그저 미소만 짓고 있었어.<end>"

## 2750003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec275-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、とてもなつかしいその笑顔が<line-break>どことなく別れを告げているように<line-break>感じられたの。不思議よね。<line-break>それでいて、あいつは夢のなかで<line-break>最後にこう言ったのよ。<line-break>幸せになれって。<end>"
EN: "But that familiar smile somehow felt like a farewell. Strange, isn't it? And yet, at the very end of the dream, he told me to be happy.<end>"
KO: "하지만 너무나 그리운 그 미소가 어딘가 작별을 고하는 것처럼 느껴졌어. 이상하지. 그러면서도 그 녀석은 꿈속에서 마지막에 이렇게 말했어. 행복해지라고.<end>"

## 2750004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec275-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれっ？<line-break>急に、涙がこぼれて…。<line-break>どうしたのかしら、あたし。<end>"
EN: "Huh? I'm crying all of a sudden... What's wrong with me?<end>"
KO: "어라? 갑자기 눈물이 흘러서… 나 왜 이러지?<end>"

## 2750005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec275-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、<value:$28>。<line-break>変なところ見せちゃって。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、なんだか泣いてすっきりしたわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. I didn't mean for you to see me like this. But somehow, I feel better after crying.<end>"
KO: "미안해, <value:$28>. 이상한 모습 보여 버렸네. 그래도 울고 나니 속이 시원해졌어.<end>"

## 2750006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec275-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>と会えてよかった。　<line-break>あたしひとりだけなら、きっと<line-break>無力感にさいなまれ、迷子みたいに<line-break>闇のなかで途方に暮れていたと思う。<line-break>もう一度、言わせて<value:$28>。<line-break>ありがとう…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm so glad I met you, <value:$28>. If I'd been all alone, I think the helplessness would have consumed me, leaving me lost in the dark with no idea where to turn. Let me say it once more, <value:$28>. Thank you...<end>"
KO: "고마워. <value:$28>를 만나서 다행이야. 나 혼자였다면 분명 무력감에 시달리며 길 잃은 아이처럼 어둠 속에서 막막해했을 거야. 다시 한 번 말하게 해 줘, <value:$28>. 고마워….<end>"

## 2760001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふたりとも、いいのか？<line-break>わしは、結婚式の立ち会いなんて<line-break>数えるほどしかやっておらんぞ？<line-break>…なんせ、アルノートゥンは<line-break>男ばっかりで<line-break>世界一、結婚と縁のない町だからな。<line-break>しかも本当に構わんのか、この格好で？<end>"
EN: "Are you both sure about this? I've only officiated at a handful of weddings in my life. Alnortun is a town full of men, after all―the place has less to do with marriage than anywhere else in the world. And are you really sure you don't mind me dressed like this?<end>"
KO: "둘 다 정말 괜찮겠나? 나는 결혼식 입회 같은 건 손에 꼽을 만큼밖에 안 해 봤다네. 알노툰은 남자뿐이라 세계에서 결혼과 가장 인연 없는 마을이니까. 게다가 정말 이 차림으로 괜찮겠어?<end>"

## 2760002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、うー、まあそのなんだ。<line-break>小難しいことは省略して、<line-break>お主らは永遠の愛を誓い合うか？<end>"
EN: "Er, ah... well, then. Let's dispense with all the complicated formalities. Do you vow to love each other forever?<end>"
KO: "아, 음, 뭐 그게 말이지. 어려운 이야기는 생략하고, 너희 둘은 영원한 사랑을 맹세하느냐?<end>"

## 2760003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誓う。<end>"
EN: "I do.<end>"
KO: "맹세해.<end>"

## 2760004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誓います。<end>"
EN: "I do.<end>"
KO: "맹세합니다.<end>"

## 2760005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにふたりを生涯の伴侶と認める。<line-break>神だろうと英雄だろうと邪神だろうと<line-break>お主らの仲は引き裂けまい。<line-break>それだけはわしが保証しよう。<line-break>以上！　ふたりの未来に幸あれ！<end>"
EN: "I hereby recognize you as partners for life. No god, hero, or evil god shall tear you apart. That much I can guarantee. That's all! May happiness await you both!<end>"
KO: "여기서 두 사람을 평생의 반려자로 인정한다. 신이든 영웅이든 사신이든 너희 사이를 갈라놓지는 못할 게야. 그것만은 내가 보증하지. 이상! 두 사람의 앞날에 행복이 있기를!<end>"

## 2760006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしたちは新しい大陸に渡るわ。<line-break>どんな世界が待っていようと、<line-break>ふたりで切り開いていけるから。<end>"
EN: "We're leaving for a new continent. Whatever kind of world awaits us, the two of us can carve out a path together.<end>"
KO: "우리는 새로운 대륙으로 갈 거야. 어떤 세계가 기다리고 있어도 둘이 함께 개척해 나갈 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 2760007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "競争だな。俺とお前、<line-break>どっちの名声が先に相手の耳に届くか。<line-break>楽しみにしている。<end>"
EN: "Let's make it a race: will word of my fame reach you first, or word of yours reach me? I look forward to finding out.<end>"
KO: "경쟁이군. 나와 너, 누구의 명성이 먼저 상대 귀에 들어갈지. 기대하고 있겠다.<end>"

## 2760008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec276-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベントED1用開終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질리온&이샤 이벤트 ED1용 시작·종료<end>"

## 2770001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "教えて、ジリオン…。<line-break>あたしはどうすればいいの？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, Zillion... What am I supposed to do?<end>"
KO: "가르쳐 줘, 질리온…. 난 어떻게 해야 해?<end>"

## 2770002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしはこれまでずっと<line-break>陰謀と裏切りの世界に生きてきたわ。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしがテジャワの変に加担したから、<line-break>多くの人が死に、そして…。<line-break>アトレイア様の目は光を失った。<line-break>あたしはいろんな人を不幸にしてきたのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そんなあたしに<value:$28>から<line-break>愛される資格なんて…。<end>"
EN: "I've spent my whole life surrounded by plots and betrayal. Because I took part in the Tejava Incident, many people died, and... Lady Atleia lost her sight. I've brought misery to so many people. Someone like me has no right to be loved by <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "난 지금까지 줄곧 음모와 배신의 세계에서 살아왔어. 내가 테자와의 변에 가담해서 많은 사람이 죽었고, 그리고…. 아트레이아 님의 눈은 빛을 잃었어. 난 여러 사람을 불행하게 만들었어. 그런 내가 <value:$28>에게 사랑받을 자격이 있을 리가….<end>"

## 2770003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　愛に資格なんて必要ない。<line-break>　　　もう自分を責めるのはよせ。<line-break>　　自分を許し、過去を忘れるんだ。<end>"
EN: "No one needs to qualify for love. Stop blaming yourself. Forgive yourself, and let go of the past.<end>"
KO: "사랑에 자격 같은 건 필요 없어. 이제 자신을 탓하는 건 그만둬. 자신을 용서하고 과거를 잊는 거야.<end>"

## 2770004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あたしのせいであなたが…。<end>"
EN: "But because of me, you...<end>"
KO: "하지만 나 때문에 당신이….<end>"

## 2770005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　これは俺自身が選んだ道だ。<line-break>　　　他の誰にも責任はない。<end>"
EN: "This is the path I chose for myself. No one else is to blame.<end>"
KO: "이건 내가 직접 선택한 길이다. 누구에게도 책임은 없어.<end>"

## 2770006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン…。<end>"
EN: "Zillion...<end>"
KO: "질리온….<end>"

## 2770007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　お前は俺のために充分、頑張った。<line-break>　　　これからはお前自身の幸せのために<line-break>　　　　　　　　　生きろ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　大丈夫。<value:$28>なら<line-break>　　　　きっとお前を支えてくれる。<end>"
EN: "You've done more than enough for me. From now on, live for your own happiness. You'll be all right. I'm sure <value:$28> will be there to support you.<end>"
KO: "넌 나를 위해 충분히 애썼다. 이제부터는 네 자신의 행복을 위해 살아라. 괜찮아. <value:$28>라면 분명 널 지탱해 줄 거야.<end>"

## 2770009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にあたしがついていってもいいの？<line-break>あたしには消せない過去がある。<line-break>決して償いきれない罪がある。<end>"
EN: "Are you sure I can come with you? I have a past I can never erase. I can never fully atone for what I've done.<end>"
KO: "정말 내가 따라가도 괜찮아? 나에겐 지울 수 없는 과거가 있어. 결코 다 갚을 수 없는 죄가 있어.<end>"

## 2770010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…。それでもあたしは<line-break>あなたについてゆきたい。<line-break>もしも、許されるのであれば…。<end>"
EN: "But... even so, I want to go with you. If you'll let me...<end>"
KO: "하지만…. 그래도 난 너를 따라가고 싶어. 허락된다면….<end>"

## 2770011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고마워. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2770012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にこれでよかったのかい？<line-break>ジリオンの願いは…これで…<line-break>かなえられたの？<end>"
EN: "Was this really the right thing to do? Did this... fulfill Zillion's wish?<end>"
KO: "정말 이걸로 괜찮았던 걸까? 질리온의 소원은… 이걸로… 이루어진 거야?<end>"

## 2770013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。ならいいんだ…。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then that's all right...<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 됐어….<end>"

## 2770014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec277-part99.toml

EN: ""

## 2780001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec278-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>と出会ってから、<line-break>俺はいろんなことに気づかされた。<line-break>　<line-break>自分を犠牲にすることの意味、<line-break>俺を想ってくれる人の願い、<line-break>幸せのありかた…。<end>"
EN: "Since meeting <value:$28>, I've come to understand so much: what it means to sacrifice myself, the wishes of anyone who cares about me, and what happiness can be...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>와 만나고 나서 나는 많은 걸 깨달았다. 자신을 희생한다는 의미, 나를 생각해 주는 사람의 바람, 행복이라는 것….<end>"

## 2780002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec278-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前と旅したお陰で<line-break>己の命を捧げることだけが<line-break>唯一の答えではないと俺は知った。<line-break>自分すら大切にできない人間には<line-break>他者を救うことなどできない。<line-break>お前はそう気づかせてくれた。<line-break>感謝している。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Traveling with you taught me that laying down my life isn't the only answer. You can't save anyone else if you can't even value yourself. You made me realize that. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "너와 여행한 덕분에 내 목숨을 바치는 것만이 유일한 답은 아니라는 걸 알았다. 자기 자신조차 소중히 여기지 못하는 인간은 타인을 구할 수도 없다. 네가 그걸 깨닫게 해 줬다. 고맙다. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2780003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec278-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それでもなお俺はイーシャを<line-break>このままにしてはおけない。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ世界中を探してでも<line-break>イーシャをもとに戻す方法を<line-break>俺は必ず見つけだす。<end>"
EN: "But even so, I can't leave Iria as she is. Even if I have to search the whole world, I will find a way to restore her.<end>"
KO: "그래도 나는 이샤를 이대로 둘 수 없어. 세계를 전부 뒤져서라도 이샤를 원래대로 되돌릴 방법을 반드시 찾아내겠어.<end>"

## 2780004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec278-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、それは自分を犠牲にして<line-break>救うためじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>イーシャを解放し、そして俺も<line-break>笑って<value:$28>と<line-break>未来を歩むためだ。<end>"
EN: "But I won't save her by sacrificing myself. I'll free Iria so that I can smile too and walk into the future with <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그건 나를 희생해서 구하기 위해서가 아니야. 이샤를 해방하고, 나도 웃으면서 <value:$28>과 미래를 걷기 위해서야.<end>"

## 2780005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec278-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長い旅になるかもしれない…。<line-break>それでも俺についてきてくれるのか？<end>"
EN: "It may be a long journey... Will you still come with me?<end>"
KO: "긴 여행이 될지도 몰라… 그래도 나를 따라와 줄 건가?<end>"

## 2780006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec278-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジリオン＆イーシャイベントED3用開終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질리온&이샤 이벤트 ED3용 시작·종료<end>"
